### [1] [Hardware-Software Co-Design for Float16 On-Device Training on RISC-V Single-Core](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21130)
*Benjamin Hubinet,Pierre-Alain Moellic,Olivier Savry,Olivier Potin,Jean-Baptiste Rigaud*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于RISC-V标准扩展Zfh和Zvfh的开源框架，实现了在资源受限的RISC-V单核处理器上的完整端侧训练，相比float32减少了约50%的内存占用，性能损失极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限的嵌入式设备上实现完整的深度神经网络训练面临内存占用过大的挑战，而传统的float32精度要求较高的内存开销。本文旨在利用RISC-V的半精度浮点扩展来降低内存需求，同时保持模型性能。

Method: 基于RISC-V的Zfh（标量半精度浮点）和Zvfh（向量半精度浮点）标准扩展，在开源框架AIfES上实现端侧训练能力。框架支持迁移学习和微调场景，包含层冻结功能以优化训练过程。在RV64GC超标量乱序FPGA软核上验证了硬件实现的可行性。

Result: 相比使用float32，内存占用减少约50%，模型性能下降极小。Zfh扩展在RV64GC超标量乱序FPGA软核上（175MHz）的面积开销很低，仅增加1.15%的LUT6和0.05%的FF。讨论了同一RISC-V核心内Zvfh实现的架构。

Conclusion: 利用RISC-V的半精度浮点扩展可以有效实现资源受限设备上的端侧训练，显著降低内存占用且硬件面积开销小，为嵌入式系统的深度学习训练提供了可行的开源解决方案。

Abstract: By leveraging standard RISC-V extensions, namely Zfh (scalar float16) and Zvfh (vector float16), this work proposes an open-source framework to enable complete on-device training on resource-constrained RISC-V single-core. Our approach allows memory footprint reduction by about 50% as compared to using float32 and with minimal model performance degradation. We also facilitate transfer learning and fine-tuning scenarios by incorporating layer-freezing capabilities. Our work builds onto AIfES, an open-source, modular and generic DNN training and inference framework for embedded systems that can be extended with custom hardware-specific functions. The benefits of float16 is further emphasized by outlining the low area overhead of Zfh on a RV64GC super-scalar out-of-order FPGA softcore (+1.15% LUT6 and +0.05% FF at 175MHz). Finally, we discuss the architecture of a Zvfh implementation within the same RISC-V core.

</details>


### [2] [Chip Floorplanning Combining Convex and Non-convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21408)
*Yilihamujiang Yimamu,Guillem Pastor-Rué,Jordi Cortadella*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于非凸和凸优化相结合的多阶段固定轮廓布图规划框架，在MCNC、GSRC和HB+基准测试上实现了业界领先的线长质量，平均HPWL相比现有最优布图规划器减少至少1%和5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 布图规划是VLSI物理设计的关键早期阶段，其质量直接影响互连线长、芯片性能和下游设计效率，因此需要开发高效的优化方法来提升布图规划质量。

Method: 提出三阶段框架：1)初始布图规划阶段采用二次规划优化模块连接性，生成具有强网络聚类的拓扑感知初始配置；2)全局布图规划阶段使用基于Adam的投影梯度法求解最小化线长和重叠的非凸优化问题；3)合法化阶段采用对数变换模型，利用水平和垂直约束图在凸规划框架下消除残留重叠。

Result: 在MCNC、GSRC和HB+基准测试上实现了业界领先的线长质量，与现有最优布图规划器相比，平均HPWL分别减少至少1%和5%。

Conclusion: 该多阶段布图规划框架通过结合非凸和凸优化技术，有效平衡了拓扑结构生成、全局优化和合法化过程，为VLSI物理设计提供了高质量的布图规划解决方案。

Abstract: Floorplanning is a critical early stage of VLSI physical design, and its quality directly impacts interconnect wirelength, chip performance, and downstream design efficiency. This article presents a multi-stage fixed-outline floorplanning framework that combines non-convex and convex optimization. The framework operates in three successive phases. In the initial floorplanning phase, a quadratic placement optimizes module connectivity to produce a topology-aware starting configuration with strong net clustering, albeit with significant module overlaps. In the global floorplanning phase, Adam-based projected gradient method is employed to solve non-convex optimization that minimizes wirelength and overlap. In the legalization phase, a log-transformed model exploiting horizontal and vertical constraint graphs eliminates residual overlaps under a convex formulation. Experiments on the MCNC, GSRC and HB+ benchmarks demonstrate that the framework achieves state-of-the-art wirelength quality, reducing average HPWL by at least 1\% and 5\% compared to state-of-the-art floorplanners on tested benchmarks.

</details>

### [3] [PortLBM: A Portable Lattice Boltzmann Tool Leveraging SYCL on AMD, NVIDIA, and Intel GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20650)
*Alexander Strack,Marcel Graf,Alexander Van Craen,Dirk Pflüger*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了基于SYCL的可移植格子玻尔兹曼框架PortLBM，在NVIDIA、AMD和Intel GPU上评估了不同数据布局和算法变体的性能，发现不同硬件需针对性调优，验证了可移植LBM软件的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着摩尔定律停滞，高性能计算转向GPU加速器，需要既能保证跨硬件平台可移植性又能确保效率的编程模型，而现有的格子玻尔兹曼方法实现缺乏这种跨平台的性能可移植性支持。

Method: 开发了基于SYCL构建的PortLBM框架，集成跨平台GPU支持和交互式实时可视化功能，支持从卡门涡街、翼型流动到多孔介质等多种仿真场景；设计了三种数据布局（stream、bundle、collision）和四种算法变体，在NVIDIA、AMD和Intel当代GPU架构上进行性能可移植性研究。

Result: 没有单一配置能在所有GPU厂商上实现最优性能，验证了系统特定调优的必要性；stream布局最大化带宽，在当代NVIDIA和Intel GPU上表现最佳；bundle布局提升缓存效率，在AMD GPU上表现优异；双格子方案实现更高吞吐量，单格子方案在内存受限场景下更优。

Conclusion: 研究强调了在日益异构的计算环境中，需要可适应、可移植的LBM软件，不同硬件架构需要针对性的配置优化以实现最佳性能。

Abstract: The lattice Boltzmann method (LBM) is a well-established approach for simulating fluid flows at the mesoscopic scale. With the stagnation of Moore's law, high-performance computing has shifted toward GPU accelerators, necessitating programming models that ensure both portability and efficiency across diverse hardware platforms.
  We present PortLBM, an extensible portable LBM framework built on SYCL that integrates cross-platform GPU support with interactive real-time visualization. PortLBM supports diverse simulation scenarios ranging from Kármán vortex streets and wing flows to porous media, and is designed for easy extension with new algorithms and backends. As part of a performance portability study, we evaluate PortLBM on contemporary GPU architectures from NVIDIA, AMD, and Intel, examining the impact of three data layouts (stream, bundle, and collision) and four algorithmic variants on simulation throughput.
  Our results show that no single configuration achieves optimal performance across all GPU vendors, confirming the need for system-specific tuning. The stream layout maximizes bandwidth and performs best on the contemporary NVIDIA and Intel GPUs, while the bundle layout improves cache efficiency and excels on the AMD GPU. Two-lattice schemes achieve higher throughput while one-lattice schemes are preferable under memory constraints. Our work underscores the necessity for adaptable, portable LBM software in increasingly heterogeneous computing environments.

</details>


### [4] [A Framework for Reputation Aware Uninorm-driven Consensus Algorithms for Blockchain Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20700)
*Bruno Ramos-Cruz,Javier Andreu-Perez,David Richerby,Luis Martínez*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于直觉模糊集和Uninorm聚合操作的声誉共识算法新方法，解决了传统共识机制中的权力集中和参与者排斥问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有区块链共识机制存在计算资源消耗大、权益要求高的问题，导致权力集中和参与者排斥，基于声誉的共识算法中声誉值存在不确定性，缺乏精确知识表示方法。

Method: 采用直觉模糊集（IFSs）表达声誉值以处理不确定性，利用Uninorm聚合操作（UAOs）监测声誉随时间变化，强化正面和负面声誉的重要性，使验证者能在后续验证过程中纠正过去的失败。

Result: 该方法保持线性计算复杂度，不引入额外通信开销，实验结果表明性能和评估效果得到改善，促进区块链网络的公平性和包容性。

Conclusion: 基于直觉模糊集和Uninorm聚合操作的声誉共识方法有效解决了传统共识机制的中心化问题，实现了更公平、更包容的区块链共识算法设计。

Abstract: The operation of blockchain is governed by consensus algorithms (CA). Several consensus mechanisms require significant computational power, while others necessitate high amounts of stakes to select the participant to validate and verify the transactions in the block, leading to centralisation of power and participant exclusion. This paper proposes a novel methodology to address these issues in reputation-based consensus algorithms by studying the reputation behaviour of the validator using intuitionistic fuzzy sets (IFSs) and uninorm aggregation operations (UAOs). Our approach uses IFSs to express the "reputation" because the reputation values in a consensus algorithm eventually imply uncertainty, and IFSs facilitate the representation of a lack of precise knowledge about reputation. Moreover, this methodology utilises uninorm aggregation operations to monitor reputation over time and reinforces the importance of negative and positive reputation. Consequently, this solution allows validators to rectify past failures in subsequent verification processes and foster an equitable consensus algorithm design. The proposed framework maintains linear computational complexity and does not introduce additional communication overhead beyond the underlying consensus protocol. Supported by experimental results, our methodology demonstrates improved performance and evaluation, promising advancements in blockchain network fairness and inclusivity.

</details>


### [5] [The Consensus Number of Untraceable Cryptocurrencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20929)
*Christian Cachin,David Lehnherr,Juan Villacis,François-Xavier Wicht*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文对比分析了两类发送者不可追踪加密货币方案的共识特性：线性方案(LUAT)共识数为2，恒定方案(CUAT)共识数随掩码集大小增长，两者在存储、同步和公平性上各有代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有加密货币发送者不可追踪方案分为两类：经典方案保留整个掩码集并添加空值标记，导致账本持续增长；恒定状态方案则消耗并替换整个集合。作者旨在探究这种设计选择如何影响同步特性。

Method: 将两类设计形式化为线性不可追踪资产转移对象(LUAT)和恒定不可追踪资产转移对象(CUAT)，并将它们定位到共识层次结构中。引入冲突图来形式化CUAT中掩码集的交集关系，分析不同不可追踪性强度下的共识特性。

Result: LUAT的共识数为2(独立于掩码集大小)，具有无饥饿特性；可通过固定掩码集分区进行垃圾回收。弱不可追踪性下CUAT共识数无界；强不可追踪性下共识数精确确定且随掩码集大小二次增长，但不具有无饥饿特性。

Conclusion: 两种对象为相同隐私付出了不同代价：LUAT以存储为代价，CUAT以同步和公平性为代价。这为加密货币隐私方案的设计选择提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Sender untraceability hides the account spent by a cryptocurrency transfer among a set of candidates, its masking set. What a transfer does to that set separates two designs: classical schemes retain the whole set and append a nullifier marking the spent account, so the ledger grows with every transfer; constant-state schemes instead consume and replace the entire set. We ask how this choice affects synchronization.
  We formalize the two designs as the linear and constant untraceable asset transfer objects (LUAT and CUAT) and locate them in the consensus hierarchy. In LUAT, transfers from distinct accounts commute. Its consensus number is 2, compared with 1 for standard asset transfer, independently of the masking-set size and of the untraceability notion, and LUAT is starvation-free. Partitioning the accounts into fixed masking sets lets exhausted sets be garbage-collected without increasing that number.
  In CUAT, a transfer consumes and replaces every account of its masking set, so two transfers whose sets intersect cannot both take effect. We formalize this with the conflict graph on masking sets, whose edges join sets sharing an account. Under weak untraceability, which protects a transaction in isolation, the consensus number is unbounded already for one-round protocols. Under strong untraceability, which protects against an observer of the complete history, untraceability holds on a history exactly when any two accounts sharing a masking set occur in the same number of the masking sets in it. This uniform incidence bounds the conflict graph, and matching constructions attain it, so the consensus number is determined exactly and grows quadratically in the masking-set size. Finally, CUAT is not starvation-free. The two objects therefore pay for the same privacy differently: LUAT in storage, CUAT in synchronization and fairness.

</details>


### [6] [Multimmit: Extending Blocks for Faster Finality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21021)
*Andrew Lewis-Pye,Patrick O'Grady*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Multimmit是一种新型SMR协议，通过多链数据分发和相对投票机制，在保持高吞吐量的同时实现了优雅的故障降级，避免了现有方案中因单个故障生产者导致的系统性能急剧下降问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有并行区块链SMR协议存在关键权衡：认证方法需要仲裁确认区块可用性，增加了消息延迟；非认证方法允许立即引用区块，但在关键路径上获取数据时性能急剧下降；最先进的Raptr协议通过最终确认仲裁持有的最长前缀来平衡，但仍对顺序敏感——单个早期批次的数据被 withheld 时，提案几乎无法最终确认，故障生产者仍能阻止系统走乐观路径。

Method: 提出Multimmit协议，适用于n≥5f+1个处理器，结合每视图仅需一轮投票的共识层与多链数据分发。投票相对于领导者提案进行，报告每个链 voters 能支持多远，并可证明超出提案的新区块。投票机制使得共识流量独立于交易量（每视图仅数十KB）。

Result: 在时间t分发的交易区块期望在t+3δ内排序，最佳情况t+2δ（从区块分发而非领导者提案开始计时）。故障下的性能降级优雅：故障生产者仅延迟自己链的区块，其他链最多等待一个视图的排序。领导者无法在最终确认其领导者区块的同时排除诚实链上新近广泛传播的区块。

Conclusion: Multimmit成功解决了现有SMR协议中故障生产者影响全局性能的问题，实现了高吞吐量与优雅降级的平衡，为现代区块链系统提供了更robust的并行处理方案。

Abstract: To meet the throughput demands of modern blockchain systems, protocols for State Machine Replication (SMR) increasingly have many processors disseminate blocks of transactions in parallel, with consensus then establishing a total ordering on the blocks of all producers. Such designs face a choice as to when a block may enter the ordering. Certified approaches wait for a quorum to attest a block's availability, which is robust but adds message delays to every transaction. Uncertified approaches let proposals reference blocks immediately, which is fast but degrades rapidly when referenced data must be fetched on the critical path. Raptr, the state of the art, takes a middle course, finalising the longest prefix of the leader's proposal that a quorum holds, so that no processor ever blocks or fetches. The remaining weakness is sensitivity to order: if the data behind a single early batch is withheld, the proposal finalises little or nothing, so individual faulty producers can still deny the system its optimistic path.
  We present Multimmit, a protocol for $n \ge 5f+1$ processors combining a consensus layer requiring one round of voting per view with multi-chain data dissemination. Votes are cast relative to the leader's proposal, reporting per chain how far the voter can support it, and may themselves attest fresh blocks beyond it. A transaction block disseminated at time $t$ is ordered by $t+3δ$ in expectation and $t+2δ$ at best, measured from the block's dissemination rather than the leader's proposal. Degradation under faults is graceful: a faulty producer delays only its own chain's blocks, costing other chains at most a one-view wait for placement. No leader can both finalise its leader block and exclude a fresh, well-circulated block of an honest chain. Consensus traffic is tens of kilobytes per view, independent of transaction volume.

</details>


### [7] [Solving Large Traveling Salesman Problems (TSPs) by a Recursive Clustering Algorithm and a Scalable FPGA-Based Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21182)
*Junqi Huang,Hawraa Abbas Almurieb,T. Nandha Kumar,Haider A. F. Almurib,Fabrizio Lombardi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于递归聚类和FPGA硬件实现的TSP求解方法，通过将城市分解为小型簇并结合模拟退火算法，实现了高可扩展性和低资源消耗的路径规划方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旅行商问题（TSP）在众多应用中具有关键作用，持续吸引研究关注。现有方法在处理大规模城市时面临可扩展性和硬件资源限制的挑战，需要一种能够高效处理大量城市且具有良好扩展性的解决方案。

Method: 提出递归聚类方法将城市划分为包含最多5个城市及其中心点的有限数量簇；在簇内使用受限TSP和模拟退火技术进行路由，以相邻簇中心点作为进出点；簇间连接通过模拟退火建立；基于FPGA实现硬件架构，利用存储器存储簇信息使方法独立于逻辑硬件；采用平方欧氏距离进行近似距离计算以降低资源利用。

Result: FPGA实现证明了该方法可路由大量城市，可扩展性仅取决于FPGA存储容量而非逻辑硬件；平方欧氏距离具有较低资源利用率；与已有方法相比，该方法可处理更多城市路由，具有更好的可扩展性和更高的工作频率。

Conclusion: 所提出的递归聚类结合FPGA硬件实现的方法为大规模TSP问题提供了一种可行且高效的解决方案，在可扩展性、运行频率和资源利用方面优于传统方法，具有良好的实际应用前景。

Abstract: The Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP) continues to attract significant research interest due to its critical role in various applications. This paper introduces a recursive clustering approach that divides cities into a limited number of clusters, each containing up to five cities and its own centroid. Constrained TSP and simulated annealing techniques are employed to route cities within each cluster, using the centroids of neighboring clusters as entry and exit points for the routing process. This method offers the benefit of producing accurate and cost-effective routing solutions, due to the reduced number of cities in each cluster. The connections between cluster centroids are established using simulated annealing. The FPGA-based proposed hardware implementation demonstrates the feasibility of routing a large number of cities, as the approach leverages memory to store cluster information. Consequently, the method is independent of the FPGA's logic hardware, and its scalability depends on the FPGA's memory capacity. Furthermore, distance calculations using approximate methods for the FPGA execution are compared and the squared Euclidean is chosen due to its low resources' utilization. Compared to previous methods, the proposed approach can handle more cities for routing, offering better scalability and a higher operating frequency.

</details>


### [8] [Controlled Periodic Synchronization for Efficient Data-Parallel Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21224)
*Imane Ettifouri,Mostapha Zbakh,Claude Tadonki*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为CPDP的数据并行训练策略，通过控制同步频率在广域网环境下实现了比传统DDP更高的测试精度和更优的时间-精度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准分布式数据并行(DDP)在每次迭代时同步梯度，在地理分布式环境中对通信延迟和网络变化高度敏感；现有的周期性方法(如LocalSGD)虽然减少了同步频率，但主要依赖参数平均，在工作者轨迹分歧时可能不足。需要研究同步频率作为通信受限分布式训练的系统参数。

Method: 提出受控周期性数据并行(CPDP)策略，兼容PyTorch-DDP，交替进行本地更新和包含梯度AllReduce与SlowMo参数平均的协调步骤。在Grid'5000平台进行实验，包括站内集群和跨越南希与索菲亚的广域网部署(16.6ms RTT)，使用ResNet-50/CIFAR-100、ViT-S/CIFAR-100和ResNet-50/TinyImageNet进行评估。

Result: 在ResNet-50/CIFAR-100固定学习率0.1设置下，CPDP达到最高峰值测试精度。K=2时，CPDP比DDP提高2.28个百分点但增加运行时间；K=4时，CPDP比DDP提高2.44个百分点并减少平均运行时间13.8%。K=4时的同步时间约为DDP的一半。在ViT-S/CIFAR-100和ResNet-50/TinyImageNet上，CPDP相比DDP保持竞争力，总体优于LocalSGD。

Conclusion: 同步频率是通信受限分布式训练的实用控制参数。CPDP通过结合梯度AllReduce和参数平均的协调步骤，在广域网环境下实现了更好的精度-时间权衡，为地理分布式训练提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Data-parallel training relies on frequent gradient synchronization across workers. Standard DDP synchronizes gradients at every iteration, which is effective on fast local-area networks but increasingly sensitive to communication latency and network variability in geographically distributed environments. Periodic methods such as LocalSGD reduce synchronization frequency but rely mainly on parameter averaging, which may be insufficient when worker trajectories diverge. This paper studies synchronization frequency as a systems parameter for communication-constrained distributed training. We evaluate Controlled Periodic Data Parallelism (CPDP), a PyTorch-DDP-compatible strategy that alternates local updates with a reconciliation step combining gradient AllReduce and SlowMo parameter averaging. Experiments are conducted on Grid'5000 across intra-site clusters and a cross-site WAN deployment spanning Nancy and Sophia with 16.6 ms RTT. In the main fixed learning-rate setting of 0.1 on ResNet-50/CIFAR-100, CPDP achieves the highest peak test accuracy among the evaluated configurations. At K=2, CPDP improves over DDP by 2.28 percentage points but incurs additional wall-clock time. At K=4, CPDP improves over DDP by 2.44 percentage points while reducing average wall-clock time by 13.8%. Direct profiling shows that exposed synchronization time at K=4 is roughly half that of DDP, explaining the improved WAN accuracy-time trade-off. Additional experiments on ViT-S/CIFAR-100 and ResNet-50/TinyImageNet show that CPDP remains competitive with DDP and generally improves over LocalSGD. Overall, the results show that synchronization frequency is a practical control parameter for distributed training under communication constraints.

</details>

### [9] [Cross-Domain Generalization in Optical Networks via Joint Contrastive and Classification Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20666)
*Ali Al Housseini,Carlos Natalino,Paolo Monti,Omran Ayoub*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种联合对比与分类学习的表征学习方法，解决了光网络中机器学习模型在异构网络域间的泛化问题，实现了光路传输质量估算的快速适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在光网络中，机器学习模型在异构网络域间的鲁棒性仍然是一个开放性挑战。在特定拓扑或运营配置数据上训练的模型，在未见过的网络中部署时往往表现出性能下降。模型缺乏跨域泛化能力。

Method: 提出了一种表征学习技术，旨在捕获跨域稳定的任务相关关系。该技术基于一种新颖的联合对比与分类学习方法，其中表征学习和任务优化同时进行，使两个目标共同塑造潜在空间。

Result: 在代表性用例——光路传输质量估算上的实验结果表明，与基线方法相比，该方法具有显著的有效性。即使在有限的微调条件下，该方法也能展现出快速适应能力并提供优异性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的联合对比与分类学习框架成功解决了光网络机器学习模型的跨域泛化问题，实现了快速适应和优异性能，为异构网络环境中的模型部署提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: The robustness of machine learning techniques across heterogeneous network domains remains an open challenge in optical networks. Models trained on data from a specific topology or operational configuration often exhibit degraded performance when deployed in unseen networks. In this work, we address this challenge by proposing a representation learning technique aimed at capturing task-relevant relationships that remain stable across domains. The proposed technique is based on a novel joint contrastive and classification learning approach in which representation learning and task optimization are performed simultaneously, allowing both objectives to shape the latent space. Experimental results on a representative use case, namely, lightpath quality of transmission estimation, demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach compared to baseline approaches, and highlight its capacity for rapid adaptation, providing excellent performance even with limited fine-tuning.

</details>


### [10] [Update the Unseen Only: Minimizing AoI for Collaborative Perception through Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20967)
*Yanan Ma,Zhuoyi Zhao,Zhengru Fang,Haonan An,Xianhao Chen,Yuguang Fang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对协同感知中的数据陈旧问题，提出了考虑车辆本地感知的移动感知AoI最小化框架LocMW，在降低时间平均AoI和提升感知精度方面均显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AoI最小化策略不适用于协同感知系统，因为它们忽略了车辆的AoI不仅通过基站更新减小，还通过车辆本地感知减小这一事实，且有限带宽会导致严重的共享数据陈旧。

Method: 推导了考虑车辆动态感知范围和变化车群的长期时间平均和AoI的闭式表达式；提出了本地感知感知最大权重调度算法(LocMW)，这是一种在线学习算法，适用于未知环境统计和延迟观测下的传感器信息广播。

Result: LocMW算法相比最优静态随机化基准实现了次线性累积超额AoI；在车辆轨迹数据集和3D感知任务上的仿真表明，LocMW将时间平均和AoI降低高达31.6%，mAP检测精度提升高达16.3%。

Conclusion: 所提出的移动感知AoI最小化框架通过显式考虑车辆的动态感知范围，有效解决了协同感知系统中的数据陈旧问题，在理论性能保证和实际感知性能提升方面均取得了显著成效。

Abstract: While collaborative perception (CP) enhances the safety of autonomous driving, limited bandwidth can cause severe shared data staleness in CP systems. Existing age-of-information (AoI) minimization policies are not well-suited for CP, as they overlook the fact that a vehicle's AoI decreases not only through updates from the source (i.e., a base station) but also through the vehicle's local sensing. To address this issue, we propose a mobility-aware AoI minimization framework for CP that explicitly accounts for vehicles' dynamic sensing ranges. We first derive a closed-form expression for the long-term time average sum AoI within a considered region, accommodating an ever-changing vehicle population and their dynamic sensed areas. Based on this characterization, we develop Local-sensing-aware Max-Weight Scheduling (LocMW), an online learning algorithm designed for sensor information broadcast from a source to vehicles under unknown environmental statistics and delayed observations. We provide performance guarantees demonstrating that LocMW achieves a sublinear cumulative excess AoI compared to the optimal stationary randomized benchmark. Extensive simulations using vehicular trajectory datasets and 3D perception tasks demonstrate that our LocMW policy substantially outperforms competing baselines, reducing the time-averaged sum AoI by up to 31.6% and improving mAP detection accuracy by up to 16.3%.

</details>


### [11] [Large Language Model Assisted Intent-Based Satellite-Integrated Access and Backhaul FWA for Rural Areas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21272)
*Anselme Ndikumana,Kim Khoa Nguyen,Adel Larabi,Mohamed Cheriet*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种AI驱动的意图感知卫星集成接入与回程（IAB）方法，通过LLM将用户意图转化为网络需求，联合优化农村地区临时野外连接和固定宽带接入，在满足数据速率要求的同时显著降低能耗并最大化能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农村地区人口密度低且连接需求高度可变，存在家庭固定宽带与临时野外作业（如种植、收割、采矿）连接需求的时空矛盾。在野外作业密集期，家庭固定网络可能闲置，导致资源使用低效和能源浪费。现有农村网络难以适应这种时空波动，且运营商缺乏对用户移动性、活动模式和意图的可视性，难以协调临时与固定网络。

Method: 提出AI驱动的意图感知卫星集成接入与回程（IAB）方法：1）利用大语言模型（LLM）将用户意图转化为明确的网络需求；2）基于推断的需求，开发动态卫星IAB固定无线接入（FWA）网络方法，联合优化临时野外连接和固定宽带接入；3）构建优化问题以在满足数据速率要求的同时最大化能效；4）采用两阶段Benders分解方法求解优化问题。

Result: 仿真结果表明，该方法显著降低了能耗，同时在满足数据速率要求的前提下最大化了能效。

Conclusion: 所提出的意图感知卫星IAB方法有效解决了农村地区固定与临时连接需求的时空矛盾，通过智能推断用户意图并动态优化网络配置，实现了能源效率的显著提升，为农村网络智能化管理提供了新思路。

Abstract: Rural areas exhibit low population density and highly variable connectivity needs shaped by both household usage and field operations such as planting, harvesting, and mining. These field activities often occur in isolated locations requiring temporary connectivity, whereas rural households depend on fixed broadband. During intensive outdoor activities, household fixed networks may remain underutilized, resulting in inefficient resource use and unnecessary energy consumption. The coexistence of residential and field-based communication demands creates substantial spatial and temporal fluctuations that the current rural network cannot effectively adapt to. Limited visibility into user mobility, activity patterns, and intent makes it difficult for operators to coordinate temporary and fixed networks. To address these underexplored challenges, we propose an AI driven Intent Aware Satellite Integrated Access and Backhaul (IAB) approach to connect rural areas. In our proposal, a large language model (LLM) translates users' intents into explicit network requirements. Guided by these inferred requirements, we develop a dynamic satellite IAB based Fixed Wireless Access (FWA) network approach that jointly optimizes temporary field connectivity and fixed broadband access to maximize energy efficiency while satisfying the data rate requirement. The formulated optimization problem is solved using a two stage Benders decomposition approach. The simulation results show that our approach significantly reduces energy consumption while maximizing energy efficiency.

</details>


### [12] [Empowering Rural Areas with Multi-radio Microwave Backhaul Supported by Digital Twin for 5G IAB-based FWA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21310)
*Anselme Ndikumana,Kim Khoa Nguyen,Adel Larabi,Mohamed Cheriet*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合微波回传、5G IAB和FWA的统一多跳网络解决方案，通过数字孪生和深度Q学习优化能效策略，在满足数据速率需求的同时显著降低能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为实现农村地区的数字包容性，需要提供高容量互联网接入。由于光纤部署成本高昂，固定无线接入（FWA）成为更具吸引力的解决方案。然而，5G FWA作为单跳方案覆盖有限，多跳方案需要解决能耗随跳数增加而增加的关键挑战，这一问题在现有文献中被忽视。

Method: 提出基于物理孪生（PT）的能效微波回传方案，优化微波回传连接5G IAB-based FWA的无线电启动、服务、休眠和唤醒状态。通过数字孪生（DT）提升性能，采用深度Q学习解决DT中的优化问题，PT中使用优化求解器。在低利用率时以降低的容量运行网络以最小化能耗。

Result: 仿真结果表明，该方法在满足数据速率要求的同时有效降低了能耗。

Conclusion: 所提出的统一多跳网络解决方案结合能量优化策略，成功解决了农村覆盖扩展与能耗增加之间的矛盾，为农村地区提供了经济高效的宽带接入方案。

Abstract: For digital inclusion, high-capacity Internet access should be provided to rural areas to support a range of services and applications. Due to the high operating costs of fiber-optic deployment, Fixed Wireless Access (FWA) is becoming a more attractive internet solution for rural areas. However, 5G FWA is a one-hop solution with limited coverage. A multi-hop solution is needed for wider rural coverage. This work considers a unified solution combining long-haul microwave, 5G Integrated Access and Backhaul (IAB), and FWA to provide a multi-hop network for extended coverage and high network capacity in rural areas. A key challenge for such a network is that energy consumption increases with the number of hops, a problem that has been overlooked in the existing literature. To address this, we propose energy-efficiency microwave backhaul for IAB-based FWA as the Physical Twin (PT). We develop an energy-efficient strategy to optimize radio start-up, serving, sleeping, and wake-up states for microwave backhaul connecting 5G IAB-based FWA serving rural areas. By operating the network at reduced capacity during low utilization, we aim to minimize energy consumption. Then, we present a Digital Twin (DT) of PT to improve its performance. We solve the formulated optimization problem using deep Q-learning in DT and the optimization solver in PT. The simulation results show that our approach satisfies the data rate requirements while reducing energy consumption.

</details>


### [13] [DISCO: Distributed Spectrum Compliance and Orchestration for Scalable IoT Coexistence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21387)
*Lyes Saad Saoud,Moussa Ayyash*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为DISCO的分层架构，用于在复杂物联网部署中实现分布式频谱合规与编排，通过分离本地频谱学习与边缘级合规监管，在不集中传输决策的情况下实现了高效的频谱共享。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模物联网部署越来越需要与现有授权和非授权系统共享频谱，在流量不确定、衰落、移动性和间歇性协调等复杂环境下，现有机制（如固定功率限制、先听后说、频谱接入数据库和学习型资源分配）虽然解决了共存问题的重要方面，但缺乏一个公共控制平面来将网络级干扰风险预算转化为对大量自主设备的轻量级指导。

Method: 提出分布式频谱合规与编排（DISCO）分层架构，将本地频谱学习与边缘级合规监管以及较慢的云或非地面网络上下文适应分离开来。DISCO作为一个可部署的合规平面，监控违规统计数据，广播紧凑的治理信号，并在不集中每个传输决策的情况下调整策略的激进程度，而非作为新的强化学习优化器或法定频谱规则的替代品。

Result: 在一个30个种子的无人机共存案例研究中，DISCO实现了81.0 Mbps的平均吞吐量，比固定功率控制高73%，平均违规率为0.053，而未协调学习的违规率为0.126。由于95%置信区间[0.030, 0.076]跨越了名义目标值0.06，证据支持在目标附近进行统计监管，而非保证完全的监管合规。

Conclusion: DISCO提供了一种可部署的频谱合规解决方案，能够在接近目标违规率的情况下显著提升吞吐量性能，实现了效率与风险的平衡。研究明确讨论了部署、复杂性、采用边界和开放验证要求等实际问题，为未来频谱共存系统的设计提供了实用参考。

Abstract: Massive Internet of Things (IoT) deployments increasingly share spectrum with incumbent, licensed, and unlicensed systems under uncertain traffic, fading, mobility, and intermittent coordination. Existing mechanisms, including fixed power limits, listen-before-talk procedures, spectrum access databases, and learning-based resource allocation, address important aspects of coexistence, but they do not provide a common control plane to translate a network-wide interference risk budget into lightweight guidance for many autonomous devices. This article introduces Distributed Spectrum Compliance and Orchestration (DISCO), a hierarchical architecture that separates local spectrum learning from edge-level compliance regulation and slower cloud or non-terrestrial-network context adaptation. DISCO is not presented as a new reinforcement-learning optimizer or as a replacement for statutory spectrum rules. Its contribution is a deployable compliance plane that monitors violation statistics, broadcasts a compact governance signal, and adjusts policy aggressiveness without centralizing every transmission decision. A 30-seed UAV coexistence case study illustrates the efficiency--risk trade-off: the reported mean throughput is 81.0~Mbps, 73\% above fixed-power control, while the mean violation rate is 0.053 compared with 0.126 for uncoordinated learning. Because the 95\% confidence interval, [0.030, 0.076], crosses the nominal target of 0.06, the evidence supports statistical regulation near the target, not guaranteed regulatory compliance. Deployment, complexity, adoption boundaries, and open validation requirements are discussed explicitly.

</details>


### [14] [Out-of-Distribution Detection in Wireless Multimodal Foundation Models for 6G ISAC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21455)
*Mohammad Farzanullah,Akram Bin Sediq,Ali Afana,Melike Erol-Kantarci*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出WMFM-OOD框架，通过构建基站原型和温度缩放概率评分机制，有效检测6G网络中基础模型的分布外样本，在DeepVerse6G数据集上AUROC达0.8824，FPR95降低约17%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型在6G网络的安全关键基础设施部署中面临分布外（OOD）问题，传统模型基于封闭世界假设，在未见过的无线电环境中容易出现静默故障，威胁系统可信度。

Method: 提出WMFM-OOD框架，在联合潜在空间中构建几何基站原型以捕获有效无线电环境的流形结构，并采用温度缩放概率评分机制区分分布内样本和协变量偏移异常。

Result: 在DeepVerse6G数据集上验证，WMFM-OOD显著优于未校准基线，AUROC达到0.8824，在最优温度设置下FPR95降低约17%，在不完全中断网络可用性的前提下缓解灾难性模型故障。

Conclusion: WMFM-OOD为6G网络中基础模型的可靠部署提供了有效的OOD检测能力，增强了系统可信度，为安全关键基础设施中的数据驱动模型部署提供了可靠性保障。

Abstract: The integration of Foundation Models (FMs), such as the Wireless Multimodal Foundation Model (WMFM), into 6G networks provides a unified framework for Integrated Sensing and Communication (ISAC), leveraging generalized representations to simultaneously optimize data transmission and environmental perception. However, the deployment of such data-driven models in safety-critical infrastructure is hindered by the Out-of-Distribution (OOD) problem, which poses a fundamental threat to system trustworthiness. Standard FMs operate under a closed-world assumption, rendering them vulnerable to silent failures when deployed in unseen radio environments. To address this reliability gap and ensure trustworthy network operation, we propose WMFM-OOD, a robust metric-based OOD detection framework. Unlike traditional methods that rely on raw compatibility scores, WMFM-OOD constructs geometric Base Station (BS) Prototypes within the joint latent space to capture the manifold structure of valid radio environments. By employing a temperature-scaled probabilistic scoring mechanism, our approach effectively distinguishes between In-Distribution (ID) and covariate-shifted anomalies. We validate the framework on the DeepVerse6G dataset. Experimental results demonstrate that WMFM-OOD significantly outperforms uncalibrated baselines, achieving an Area Under the Receiver Operating Characteristic Curve (AUROC) of 0.8824 and reducing the False Positive Rate (FPR) at 95 % True Positive Rate (TPR), commonly referred to as FPR95, by approximately 17% in the optimal temperature regime, thereby providing an initial layer of detection sensitivity to mitigate catastrophic model failures without completely disrupting network availability.

</details>

### [15] [DataPrep-Bench: Benchmarking LLMs as Training Data Preparators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20465)
*Hao Liang,Qifeng Cai,Yibo Lin,Jianzhuo Du,Qifeng Xia,Sizhe Qiu,Linzhuang Sun,Meiyi Qiang,Zhaoyang Han,Xiaochen Ma,Bohan Zeng,Ruichuan An,Conghui He,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了DataPrep-Bench，首个统一评估LLM驱动数据准备的基准，涵盖数据构建和数据质量评估两大能力，并发布了Data-Construction-Skill方法和DAS评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练数据质量决定LLM能力，但缺乏统一基准来衡量LLM、智能体和数据驱动工作流端到端准备训练数据的能力。现有研究未将数据构建与数据质量评估作为同等重要的目标进行联合评估。

Method: 构建DataPrep-Bench基准，包含两个评估轨道：（1）数据构建：基于相同原始数据源，通过微调基础模型并评估下游性能来评分；（2）数据质量评估：通过评分函数与下游性能的Pearson相关性来评估。发布Data-Construction-Skill智能体和分布对齐分数(DAS)评估器。

Result: Data-Construction-Skill在Llama-3.1-8B Finance上相比Dolly基线提升近20个百分点，在知识密集型领域与最强方法相当。DAS在六个领域中四个取得最强跨模型相关性，是唯一在Math、Science和Medical同时达到r>0.70的指标，超越现有质量、多样性和启发式评估器。

Conclusion: DataPrep-Bench提供了统一、基于下游任务的框架，将数据构建和评估作为同等重要的目标进行测量，为LLM驱动数据准备研究提供了标准化的评估平台。

Abstract: The quality of training data fundamentally determines the capabilities of large language models (LLMs), yet no unified benchmark exists to measure how well LLMs, agents, and data-centric workflows actually prepare training data end to end. We view LLM-driven data preparation as comprising two complementary capabilities: data construction, which transforms raw sources into supervised training data, and data quality evaluation, which predicts the training value of candidate datasets before downstream training; throughout, "quality" refers to downstream training utility rather than surface-level textual properties. We introduce DataPrep-Bench, the first unified benchmark that jointly evaluates both capabilities under a shared downstream-grounded protocol over six domains and multiple base models. For data construction, methods consume identical raw sources and are scored by fine-tuning a base model on their outputs jointly with Dolly-15k; alongside this track we release Data-Construction-Skill, a skill-guided agent that lifts the Dolly-only baseline by nearly 20 points absolute on Llama-3.1-8B Finance and is competitive with the strongest agent- and DataFlow-based methods in knowledge-extraction-dense domains. For data quality evaluation, scoring functions are scored by Pearson correlation with downstream performance on a shared candidate pool; we release the Distributional Alignment Score (DAS), a distribution-based evaluator that uses MMD between a candidate dataset and a domain proxy. DAS attains the strongest cross-model correlation in four of six domains and is the only metric clearing r > 0.70 simultaneously in Math, Science, and Medical, outperforming existing quality-, diversity-, and heuristic-based evaluators. DataPrep-Bench provides a unified, downstream-grounded framework for measuring progress on both capabilities as co-equal targets of LLM-driven data preparation.

</details>


### [16] [PhantomFill: When the Form Demands an Answer, Language Models Invent One](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20492)
*Rana Muhammad Usman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究发现生产环境中的语言模型在填写结构化表单（如JSON字段）时会产生幻觉。当强制要求填写特定字段时，模型会编造答案，而在自由文本形式下则会诚实地表示无法回答。作者发布了PhantomFill基准测试来量化这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的语言模型主要任务不是创作文本，而是填写表单（JSON字段、函数参数、提取模板等）。然而，这种结构化输出格式本身会导致模型产生幻觉，这是一个被忽视但普遍存在的严重问题。

Method: 研究者对13个模型进行对照实验：使用无法回答的问题（如显示12,400点赞但无可见回复的帖子），仅改变答案格式。对比自由文本输出与带有必填字段的JSON格式输出，观察模型是否诚实回答"无数据"或编造答案。发布PhantomFill基准测试，引入强制编造率和逃逸利用率两个可量化指标。

Result: 自由文本下GPT-5.5有98%的概率诚实回答无数据；但当JSON要求填写情感字段时，40次测试全部编造答案。13个模型中10个在必填字段下编造率达到100%。提供"证据不足"选项仅对前沿闭源模型有效，9个开源权重模型全部忽略该选项。"不要推断情感"的明确指令在6个模型中有4个被schema覆盖。同一模型家族内，最小模型拒绝、中型模型编造、大型模型再次拒绝，表明诚实性与模型规模无线性关系。

Conclusion: 幻觉恰恰发生在无法进行模糊表达的地方：必填枚举字段和最小计数数组。修复方案仅需一行schema修改，但这种失败在生产环境中普遍存在。诚实性在格式压力下的表现是训练结果，但目前无人对此进行测量。

Abstract: Language models in production do not write prose. They fill forms: JSON fields, function arguments, extraction templates. We show that the form itself causes hallucination.
  We ask thirteen models the same question about the same input and change only the answer format. The inputs are built so the question cannot be answered: a viral post showing 12,400 likes but no visible replies, a support ticket whose call was never transcribed. In free text, GPT-5.5 answers honestly. It says there is no reply data, 98% of the time. Given a required JSON field for sentiment, the same model invents an answer 40 times out of 40. It fabricates the mood of crowds it never saw and quotes customers it never heard.
  The pattern holds with force. Required fields drive fabrication to 100% in ten of thirteen models. An explicit "insufficient evidence" option rescues only the frontier: all nine open-weight models ignore it. A direct instruction, do not infer sentiment, is overridden by the schema in four of six models. Resistance does not come with scale: within a single model family, the smallest model refuses, the mid-sized model fabricates, the largest refuses again. Honesty under format pressure is a training outcome that no one is measuring.
  The fabrication hides exactly where hedging is impossible: in required enums and minimum-count arrays, fields where no disclaimer fits. We release PhantomFill, a benchmark with deterministic scoring and two reportable numbers: the Coerced Fabrication Rate and the Escape Utilization Rate. The fix we test is one line of schema. The failure we measure is everywhere.

</details>


### [17] [The Active Ingredient in Muon's Grokking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20512)
*Yufeng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过消融实验发现Muon优化器在模运算grokking任务上的加速效果主要源于正交化（Newton-Schulz迭代），而非谱范数约束。正交化优化器能以更低谱范数达到泛化，但迭代次数影响稳定性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作将Muon优化器的grokking加速归因于"谱范数约束加正交化动量"，但未分离各机制的独立贡献。本研究旨在明确哪种机制真正起作用，深入理解Muon的行为特性。

Method: 采用多种方法：(1)多随机种子和学习率扫描进行消融实验，分离正交化与谱范数约束的效果；(2)机制分析探究优化器到达泛化时的谱范数特性；(3)调整Newton-Schulz迭代次数（从5降至1）测试稳定性；(4)引入稳定性感知指标，同时报告首次穿越时间和持续grokking时间。

Result: (1)加速效果完全来自正交化：仅正交化与完整Muon性能相当，仅谱范数约束与AdamW无差异且不可靠；(2)正交化优化器在约3倍更低谱范数下达到泛化，找到更低范数解而非简单减少扰动；(3)单次迭代更快但不稳定、易崩溃，五次迭代是学习率鲁棒选择；(4)谱缩放可移除且无性能损失。

Conclusion: Muon优化器的grokking加速核心机制是正交化而非谱范数约束。迭代次数需要在速度与鲁棒性间权衡。稳定性感知指标对公平比较grokking优化器至关重要，仅报告"更快"可能产生误导性结论。

Abstract: The Muon optimizer reaches the grokking threshold on modular arithmetic faster than AdamW. Prior work attributes this to "spectral-norm constraints plus orthogonalized momentum" but does not isolate which mechanism matters. To better understand Moun's behavior, we run multi-seed and multi-learning-rate sweeps to decompose and stress-test the effect. First, an ablation shows the speedup comes from orthogonalization (the Newton-Schulz iteration): orthogonalize-only matches full Muon, whereas spectral-only is no faster than AdamW and is unreliable, and this verdict holds across learning rates. Second, a mechanistic analysis finds that orthogonalizing optimizers reach generalization at roughly 3x lower spectral norm and, controlling for how much the embedding actually moves, settle into a lower-norm solution rather than simply perturbing the embedding less. Third, reducing the Newton-Schulz iteration count from five to one accelerates reaching the threshold but makes the grokked solution fragile, prone to transient collapse, with fragility that grows with learning rate; a single iteration is fast and stable only at small learning rate, while the canonical five iterations are the learning-rate-robust choice. We also show spectral scaling can be dropped at no measured cost. A methodological thread runs throughout: under a stability-aware metric, "faster" claims about grokking optimizers can invert, so we report both first-crossing and sustained-grok times. To support reproducibility, we release our full training and analysis code at https://github.com/louiswang524/muon-grokking-frontier

</details>

Conclusion: 初步研究中观察到的通道搜索信号在更大规模实验中得到验证并成功转移，密集采样不仅证实了优化趋势的有效性，还揭示了参数效率提升和架构设计规律，为基于大语言模型的神经网络架构搜索提供了更可靠的经验证据。

Abstract: Promising initial results in closed-loop large-language-model-based channel-configuration search demonstrated that neural-network widths can be optimized directly through executable code generation and accuracy feedback. The scaled run also reveals improved parameter efficiency. The best model reaches 0.3676 with 11.8M parameters, compared with an early high-performing model at 0.3144 with 166.5M parameters. Beyond accuracy, the larger sample exposes architectural regularities that were difficult to assess from sparse observations. Non-power-of-two channel widths occur in 41.8% of verified candidates, and the strongest models share structured channel-allocation patterns characterized by moderate early widths and expanded middle or later blocks. These findings indicate that the channel-search signal observed in the initial study transfers
