All measurements come from one model at 14B; the extraction is from an aligned instruction-tuned checkpoint, which leaves the structure's provenance open; and the intervention that prevents misalignment also abolishes the narrow trained behavior.

</details>


### [112] [Hilbert Operator for Progressive Encoding (HOPE): A Mathematical Framework for Deconstructing Learned Representations in Deep Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21366)
*Hossein Mobahi,Peter L. Bartlett*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了HOPE框架，将网络压缩从离散域转换到Hilbert空间，通过将神经元建模为秩-1希尔伯特-施密特算子，统一了剪枝和神经元合并，实现了无数据和无超参数的网络表示解构方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络编码了复杂表示，但解构这些内部知识仍然是一个挑战。由于学习与压缩之间存在联系，网络压缩为分析这些知识提供了有前景的视角。然而，标准压缩启发式方法常受尺度对称性和架构偏差的影响。

Method: 引入Hilbert算子渐进编码（HOPE）框架，将网络压缩从离散域转移到连续函数的Hilbert空间。通过将单个神经元建模为秩-1希尔伯特-施密特算子，将剪枝和神经元合并统一为低秩子空间投影。进一步引入宏块驱逐，在统一度量下涵盖多层结构（如整个残差路径）。

Result: HOPE是一种无数据和无超参数的框架。在模型压缩和微调方面展示了概念验证实验，突出了该理论的实用潜力。该方法实现了跨不同类型和大小层的无偏架构决策。

Conclusion: HOPE成功地将网络压缩问题转化为Hilbert空间中的数学问题，提供了一种统一的理论框架来渐进式解构训练网络权重中的表示，为理解深度神经网络的内部知识提供了新视角。

Abstract: Deep neural networks encode complex representations, but deconstructing this internal knowledge remains a challenge. Given the link between learning and compression, network compression offers a promising lens to analyze this knowledge. However, standard compression heuristics often suffer from scale symmetries and architectural biases. To resolve these, we introduce Hilbert Operator for Progressive Encoding (HOPE), a mathematical framework to gradually deconstruct the representations in trained network weights.
  HOPE shifts network compression from the discrete domain into a Hilbert space of continuous functions. By modeling individual neurons as rank-1 Hilbert-Schmidt operators, HOPE unifies pruning and neuron merging as low-rank subspace projection. Extending this formulation, HOPE introduces macro block eviction to encompass multi-layer structures like entire residual pathways under the same unified metric. This unified approach enables unbiased architectural decisions across layers with different types and sizes. HOPE is a data-free and hyperparameter-free framework. We present proof-of-concept experiments in model compression and fine-tuning to highlight the practical potential of our theory.

</details>


### [113] [Mean-to-Score Discrete Diffusion: Posterior-Mean Denoisers for Score Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21372)
*Jingyuan Li,Xiaoyi Jiang,Yixuan Jiang,Wei Liu,Yi Zhu,Zuoqiang Shi,Pipi Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现Score Entropy Discrete Diffusion (SEDD)存在贝叶斯可实现性问题，提出两种解决方案：(1)投影到bridge polytope改善性能；(2)提出mean-to-score (M2S)方法，预测清洁token后验均值并转换为分数，在CIFAR-10和OpenWebText上显著超越基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SEDD虽保证正向跳转率非负，但未确保贝叶斯可实现性：噪声状态的score ratios无需由任何清洁token后验在正向核下联合诱导。训练后的纯均匀SEDD检查点中，约四分之一的完整score vectors违反坐标约束，超过半数虽在约束内但与任何有效后验不兼容，导致有限步采样中出现负预归一化权重。

Method: 1) 提出将原始scores投影到bridge polytope的方法，消除负权重；
2) 引入mean-to-score (M2S)方法：预测清洁token后验均值，通过精确核依赖线性映射转换为score。该构造适用于满足温和支持条件的任意已知坐标级连续时间马尔可夫链(CTMC)。对于均匀损坏，它将概率单纯形映射到bridge polytope；对于吸收掩码损坏，目标函数精确恢复MD4。

Result: 1) 投影方法在采样器不变情况下将生成PPL从203.6降至175.1；
2) 在28.4M参数CIFAR-10对比实验中，M2S将测试BPD从3.173降至3.129，FID-50k显著降低；
3) 170M参数M2S模型在约262B OpenWebText token slots上训练，在所有测试采样预算下均优于纯均匀SEDD、GIDD和Neural CTMC检查点，在128步时达到生成PPL 143.3，而最强纯均匀基线为183.6。

Conclusion: SEDD存在本质的贝叶斯可实现性约束问题，通过投影和M2S两种方法可有效解决。M2S方法具有理论保证，在多个基准上显著优于现有离散扩散方法，为离散数据生成提供了新的有效框架。

Abstract: Score Entropy Discrete Diffusion (SEDD) parameterizes discrete reverse processes with unconstrained positive score ratios. While positivity guarantees nonnegative reverse jump rates, it does not ensure Bayes realizability: ratios at a noisy state need not be jointly induced by any clean-token posterior under the forward kernel. The score-entropy loss has the correct population optimum but does not enforce this constraint away from it. In a trained pure-uniform SEDD checkpoint, roughly one quarter of complete score vectors violate the coordinate box, while more than half lie inside it yet remain materially incompatible with any valid posterior. Such violations can produce negative pre-normalization weights in finite-step sampling. Projecting raw scores onto the bridge polytope removes all observed negative weights and improves external generative PPL from $203.6$ to $175.1$ without changing the sampler. We introduce \emph{mean-to-score} (M2S), which predicts a clean-token posterior mean and converts it to the score through an exact kernel-dependent linear map. The construction applies to any known coordinate-wise continuous-time Markov chain (CTMC) satisfying a mild support condition. For uniform corruption, it maps the probability simplex onto the bridge polytope; for absorbing-mask corruption, the resulting objective recovers MD4 exactly. In a controlled 28.4M-parameter CIFAR-10 comparison, M2S lowers test BPD from $3.173$ to $3.129$ and FID-50k from $\CifarSEDDFID$ to $\CifarMtwoSFID$. A 170M-parameter M2S model trained on about 262B OpenWebText token slots outperforms the evaluated pure-uniform SEDD, GIDD, and Neural CTMC checkpoints at every tested sampling budget, reaching generative PPL $143.3$ at 128 steps versus $183.6$ for the strongest pure-uniform baseline.

</details>


### [114] [A Diffusion-Model Subpopulation Digital Twin for Mobile Health Deployment: A Case Study on the HeartSteps Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21403)
*Ziping Xu,Yuyi Chang,Chenshun Ni,Nithin Sugavanam,Asim H. Gazi,Pedja Klasnja,Emre Ertin,Susan A. Murphy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了JITAI-Twins方法，通过条件时间序列扩散模型构建数字孪生，用于在即时适应性干预部署前测试和优化在线算法设计决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动健康干预中使用在线学习和决策算法来个性化推送健康行为提示，但设计不当的算法会增加用户负担并导致用户流失，因此需要在实际部署前针对真实用户模拟环境验证新的算法设计决策。

Method: 构建基于条件时间序列扩散模型的JITAI-Twins数字孪生，具有时间一致性（未来行动不影响生成的过去），通过三步训练流程实现：在大规模观察数据集上预训练、在相关人群的小规模既往干预部署中微调、利用领域专家知识进行推理时校准。

Result: 在HeartSteps系列（v2至v4）物理活动建议干预部署的预部署阶段验证表明，该方法比简单模拟器更好地重现了目标亚群的时间结构和参与者间结构，能够模拟即将开展的目标部署。

Conclusion: JITAI-Twins可用于在实际干预部署前模拟目标部署，为测试和指导在线算法设计决策提供必要支持，从而降低干预设计的风险。

Abstract: Mobile-health interventions increasingly use online learning and decision making algorithms to personalize when to nudge users toward healthier behavior, but a poorly designed algorithm can burden and disengage participants. New algorithm design decisions should therefore be vetted against realistic simulated users before each real-life deployment. We propose a method to develop ``JITAI-Twins'': digital twins of a target subpopulation for comparing candidate online algorithms before a just-in-time adaptive intervention (JITAI) deployment. The method builds on a conditional time-series diffusion model that is temporally consistent (future actions do not affect the generated past), and it supports repeated updating from three sources of information, in three steps: pre-training on a large observational dataset, fine-tuning on small prior intervention deployments in related populations, and inference-time calibration to the next target population from domain-scientist expertise. We validate the twin at each pre-deployment stage of the long-running HeartSteps series (v2 through v4) of physical-activity suggestion intervention deployments, treating each successive deployment as an upcoming study. The proposed method reproduces the target subpopulation's temporal and between-participant structure better than simpler simulators. These results suggest that our twin can be used to simulate a target deployment before it runs, the prerequisite for testing and informing online algorithm design decisions.

</details>


### [115] [Semantic-Aware Task Clustering for Constructive and Cooperative Multi-Tasking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21426)
*Ahmad Halimi Razlighi,Maximilian H. V. Tillmann,Edgar Beck,Bho Matthiesen,Armin Dekorsy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对协作多任务语义通信中合作可能具有破坏性的问题，提出了一种语义感知的任务聚类方法，通过分阶段优化实现语义对齐任务的聚类，有效避免了负迁移并提升了准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在协作多任务语义通信中，任务间的语义关系决定了协作是建设性的还是破坏性的。现有研究表明，不加区分的多任务协作可能导致性能下降，因此需要一种机制来识别语义相关的任务并确保建设性协作。

Method: 提出语义感知任务聚类方法，将问题分解为两阶段优化：(i)利用层次化密度空间聚类进行语义聚类，在初始训练后一次性完成；(ii)在发现的聚类组内进行端到端联合训练，避免跨组训练。

Result: 仿真结果表明，该方法有效缓解了破坏性合作和负迁移现象，相比未聚类的多任务训练和单独训练基线，获得了准确率提升。

Conclusion: 通过语义感知的任务聚类，可以确保只有语义对齐的任务才进行协作训练，从而有效解决多任务语义通信中的负迁移问题，为设计更可靠的协作通信系统提供了新思路。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-task semantic communication (CMT-SemCom) improves task execution performance by leveraging shared representations. However, as we demonstrated in [1], cooperative multi-tasking can be either constructive or destructive, depending on the semantic relationships among tasks. To ensure constructive cooperation, we propose a semantic-aware task clustering method for CMT-SemCom. We have formulated a sequential multi-stage optimization problem in which semantically aligned tasks are clustered once after a short initial training phase, and then end-to-end (E2E) joint training is conducted exclusively within the discovered groups. Specifically, the problem decomposes into two stages: (i) a semantic clustering problem leveraging hierarchical density-based spatial clustering, and (ii) an intra-cluster E2E CMT-SemCom learning problem. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed framework effectively mitigates destructive cooperation and negative transfer, yielding accuracy gains compared to unclustered multi-tasking and individual training baselines.

</details>


### [116] [Context-weighted Discrete Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21427)
*Daniil Cherniavskii,Daniel Severo,Karen Ullrich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合局部上下文信息的离散流匹配改进方法，通过上下文加权采样器和缩放交叉熵损失函数，显著提升了生成质量并降低了困惑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的分解训练目标使模型面临不同难度的目标，混合了条件良好、可预测的token和模糊、高熵的token。作者通过实验发现，token值的不确定性与邻域中可用上下文的密度密切相关，这促使他们将局部上下文信息纳入连续时间马尔可夫链（CTMC）框架中。

Method: 提出了两种改进方法：1）上下文加权采样器，通过修改CTMC纳入局部上下文信息，以极小的计算开销提升生成质量；2）缩放交叉熵损失函数，对来自不同token的训练信号进行重新加权，减少生成困惑度。

Result: 在OpenWebText上，生成困惑度降低了高达63%；在生成质量上匹配了强半自回归块扩散基线，同时保留了以任意顺序生成的能力。

Conclusion: 局部上下文是离散生成建模的重要因素，简单的上下文感知修改可以显著改善采样和训练效率，突显了局部上下文在离散生成模型中的重要作用。

Abstract: Discrete flow matching provides a flexible framework for generative modeling on discrete structures. However, the standard factorized training objective exposes the model to targets of varying difficulty, mixing well-conditioned, predictable tokens with ambiguous, high-entropy ones. We empirically demonstrate that the uncertainty over the value of each token is closely related to the density of available context in its neighborhood. Motivated by this observation, we propose a simple modification to the underlying continuous-time Markov chain (CTMC) that incorporates local context information. Our context-weighted sampler improves generation quality with negligible computational overhead, while our scaled cross-entropy loss function reweights the training signal from different tokens and reduces generative perplexity by up to 63% on OpenWebText. Moreover, our approach matches a strong semi-autoregressive block diffusion baseline in quality while retaining the ability to perform generation in any order. These results highlight the role of local context as an important factor in discrete generative modeling and show that simple context-aware modifications can significantly improve both sampling and training efficiency.

</details>


### [117] [KroQuant: Kronecker-Structured Block Transforms for Efficient Post-Training Quantization of Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21446)
*Yann Bouquet,Alireza Khodamoradi,Kristof Denolf,Mathieu Salzmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KroQuant是一种用于扩散变换器的W4A4后训练量化方法，通过Kronecker结构变换实现高质量低开销的激活值离群值处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散变换器在W4A4量化时，激活值中的离群值无法用4位格式表示，导致输出质量严重下降。现有解决方案在量化质量和推理成本之间存在权衡：逐通道缩放计算廉价但影响量化精度；固定Hadamard变换精度高但在线成本大；学习的全维度变换精度最佳但计算代价过高。

Method: 提出KroQuant方法，对每个32元素激活块应用学习的Kronecker结构可逆变换，参数量低于逐通道缩放的一半。块局部结构以小型张量核心GEMM运行。离线LoRaQ权重校准吸收残差权重量化误差。

Result: 在MI350 GPU上，KroQuant量化器内核比SmoothQuant内核快达14%。在PixArt-Σ、SANA和FLUX.1-schnell的W4A4 (MXFP4e2)量化下，KroQuant在MJHQ-30K和SDCI上的输出比SVDQuant和LoRaQ更接近FP参考，同时保持或提升图像质量。

Conclusion: KroQuant成功平衡了量化质量与推理效率，为扩散变换器的W4A4量化提供了高效实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) of diffusion transformers (DiTs) to W4A4 severely degrades output quality, because activations entering each linear layer contain outliers that 4-bit formats cannot represent. The standard fix applies an invertible linear transform to the activations and its inverse to the weights before quantizing both. Normalization layers between blocks force this transform to run online at every denoising step, making its inference computation cost the binding design constraint. Existing options trade quantization quality for inference cost: per-channel scaling (SmoothQuant) is computationally cheap but impacts the magnitude of the channels, which can harm quantization accuracy; fixed Hadamard transforms yield better quantization accuracy but require large block sizes that incur a high online cost; learned full-$d$ invertible transforms calibrate best but entail a prohibitive dense $d \times d$ matrix multiplication (GEMM) per layer per step.
  We propose KroQuant, a PTQ method that applies a learned Kronecker-structured invertible transform to each 32-element block of the activation, storing less than half the parameters of per-channel scaling. The block-local structure runs as small tensor-core GEMMs, and on an MI350 GPU the KroQuant quantizer kernel is up to $14\%$ faster than the SmoothQuant kernel. Offline LoRaQ weight calibration then absorbs the residual per-weight quantization error. On PixArt-$Σ$, SANA, and FLUX.1-schnell at W4A4 (MXFP4e2), KroQuant produces outputs closer to the FP reference than SVDQuant and LoRaQ on MJHQ-30K and SDCI, while preserving or improving image quality.

</details>


### [118] [Test-Time Scaling via Error Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21453)
*Rajiv Shailesh Chitale,Rahul Madhavan,Taneesh Gupta,Deepanway Ghosal,Aravindan Raghuveer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了TTEL算法，通过错误定位在推理时进行token级的信用分配，实现了在降低计算成本的同时提升大语言模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推理时计算扩展方法（如独立采样和顺序多轮细化）缺乏token级的信用分配，导致计算效率低下，因为有效的推理前缀经常被丢弃。

Method: 提出TTEL算法，通过比较在反馈信息条件下的条件概率与空上下文基线，定位错误发生的具体步骤，然后截断轨迹并在有效前缀处分支生成新内容。

Result: 在顺序推理领域建立了严格的帕累托前沿；在LiveCodeBench上使用Qwen3-8B达到71.0%的pass@64，同时生成的token数约为独立采样的一半（360.4k vs 735.0k）；在数学基准测试上显著优于竞争基线。

Conclusion: TTEL通过token级的错误定位实现了更高效的推理时计算扩展，在性能和计算成本之间取得了更优的权衡。

Abstract: Scaling inference-time computation has emerged as a reliable method to improve the performance of large language models on complex reasoning and programming tasks. However, standard approaches such as independent sampling and sequential multi-turn refinement operate without token-level credit assignment, resulting in computational inefficiency, since valid reasoning prefixes are frequently discarded. In this work, we introduce Test-Time Scaling via Error Localization (TTEL), an inference-time algorithm that utilizes fixed or environment feedback to perform token-level error localization. By comparing conditional probabilities under informed feedback against a null-context baseline, TTEL isolates the step at which an error occurred. The algorithm then truncates the trajectory and branches a new generation, maximally reusing the valid prefix. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that TTEL establishes strictly dominating Pareto frontiers across sequential reasoning domains, measured by pass-at-k vs. generated-token cost. With Qwen3-8B on LiveCodeBench, TTEL attains a pass@64 of 71.0% while generating approximately half as many tokens as independent sampling (360.4k vs. 735.0k). Generalizing to math benchmarks AIME-2025 and HMMT-2025, TTEL cleanly outperforms competing test-time baselines across both Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507.

</details>


### [119] [Error Certificates for KV-Cache Eviction via Randomized Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21475)
*Peng Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文证明确定性KV缓存驱逐无法一致估计误差，提出随机化驱逐方法实现误差归因而非预测，预注册实验验证了7项主张中的4项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有确定性KV缓存驱逐机制存在根本缺陷：保留系统无法知晓被驱逐内容的影响，导致无法建立一致的误差估计器，限制了缓存管理的可靠性。

Method: 1. 理论证明确定性驱逐的不可识别性问题；2. 提出随机化驱逐策略：使用已知包含概率的泊松采样尾部，通过logit偏移执行Hájek校正；3. 结合调查抽样方差估计器构建每步误差证书；4. 预注册7项实验主张进行验证。

Result: 1. 随机化方法实现0.97经验覆盖率且无精度损失；2. 预注册7项主张中3项失败：问题感知驱逐低成本、输出对数概率预测失败优于证书、证书门控预算升级无效；3. 证书能区分缓存导致与固有失败（AUC 0.73-0.73对比置信度0.47-0.54），更好地调度重计算。

Conclusion: 随机化驱逐换取的是归因能力而非预测能力，误差证书能有效识别缓存引起的失败并指导重计算调度，为KV缓存管理提供了理论基础和实践指导。

Abstract: Deterministic KV-cache eviction keeps the top-$k$ tokens under an importance score and deletes the rest. We prove that this design cannot know what it destroyed: evicted values can be altered so that everything the serving system retains is unchanged while the true attention-output error grows arbitrarily, so no serving-time estimator of that error is consistent. Randomized eviction restores identifiability. With a Poisson-sampled tail at known inclusion probabilities, one logit offset performs the Hájek correction inside the softmax, and a survey-sampling variance estimator over the retained set becomes a per-step error certificate with 0.97 empirical coverage at no accuracy cost. On real workloads we pre-registered seven claims and lost three: question-aware eviction at 25--50\% budgets is nearly free; output log-probability predicts failure better than the certificate; certificate-gated budget escalation adds nothing. What survives is attribution: the certificate separates cache-induced from inherent failures (AUC 0.73--0.75, against 0.47--0.54 for output confidence) and schedules recomputation better than random or confidence gating. Randomization buys attribution, not prediction.

</details>


### [120] [Finite-Sample Coverage Audits for High-Recall Candidate Generation: Certification and Learning-Theoretic Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21480)
*Martin Anthony,Kaveh Salehzadeh Nobari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了高召回率流程中验证遗漏相关项数量所需的审计标签复杂度，证明了必须对排除池进行抽样审计，并提供了有限样本工具包来证明遗漏质量、召回率等指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在经验流水线中，初始高召回率阶段决定哪些项目进入后续审查、标注或建模环节，该阶段遗漏的相关项目将永远丢失。需要研究如何用有限数量的审计标签，以有限样本有效性来证明这些遗漏的相关项质量很小。

Method: 1) 证明仅使用候选集内部标签的程序无法证明遗漏质量的任何非平凡界限，必须对排除池抽样；
2) 证明匹配的有限语料库下界，证明任何有效审计在零遗漏情况下必须检查约N₀/m个排除池标签；
3) 开发精确有限样本工具包，使用二项和超几何反演而非渐近近似。

Result: 1) 排除池审计在零遗漏情况下是极小极大率最优的；
2) 提供了证明遗漏质量、通过双池设计转换为召回率、同时证明预指定嵌套候选生成器族、针对声明扰动机制生成压力测试证书的工具包；
3) 这些证书可与可观察审查负担配对，选择满足遗漏质量目标的最小负担预指定候选生成器。

Conclusion: 所有保证遵循一个原则：候选生成器（或从中选择的预指定族）和审计规则必须在检查认证标签之前固定。排除池审计不仅是方便的，而且是遗漏质量证明的极小极大率最优方法。

Abstract: An initial high-recall stage in an empirical pipeline decides which items pass to later review, labelling, or modelling, and relevant items it misses are lost to every subsequent stage. We study how many audit labels are needed to certify, with finite-sample validity, that this missed relevant mass is small, and our main results characterise the label complexity of this problem. We first show that no procedure using only labels from inside the candidate set can certify any non-trivial bound on the missed mass: the audit must sample the excluded pool, the only region where unrecovered relevant items can lie. We then prove a matching finite-corpus lower bound. Any valid audit that certifies fewer than $m$ missed relevant items with high probability when none are present, even if adaptive and permitted to label the entire included pool, must inspect on the order of $N_0/m$ excluded-pool labels. Excluded-pool auditing is therefore minimax rate-optimal, not merely convenient, for missed-mass certification in the zero-miss regime. Building on this characterisation, we develop an exact finite-sample toolkit, using binomial and hypergeometric inversion rather than asymptotic approximation, that certifies missed mass, converts it to recall through a two-pool design, certifies pre-specified families of nested candidate generators simultaneously, and produces stress-test certificates against declared perturbation mechanisms. These certificates can be paired with observable review burden to select the least burdensome pre-specified candidate generator meeting a missed-mass target. Every guarantee holds under one discipline: the candidate generator, or the pre-specified family from which it is selected, and the audit rule are fixed before the certification labels are examined.

</details>


### [121] [Windowed-MTP: Removing the Full-Context Draft-KV Tax at Million-Token Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21535)
*Alagappan Valliappan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates autoregressive generation by having a cheap draft propose tokens that a target verifies in parallel. Frontier models increasingly ship a built-in Multi-Token-Prediction (MTP/NEXTN) draft head under the assumption that the draft is negligibly cheap. At million-token context this breaks: an MTP draft head typically runs full attention over the entire KV cache at every draft step, so its read grows linearly with context and comes to dominate the draft cost -- precisely where speculation is most valuable. The effect compounds with draft length (a deep native draft can turn net-negative, slower than no speculation) and sharpens under hybrid/linear-attention targets, where cheaper verification leaves the draft's full-attention read exposed. We apply a StreamingLLM-style sliding window plus attention sink to the draft's attention only (Windowed-MTP), leaving full-attention verification intact. It is training-free, drop-in, and lossless by construction: the full-attention target still decides every accepted token, so windowing changes only which tokens are proposed, never which are accepted. It bounds the draft's KV working set to a constant, dropping ~99% of KV entries at 1M. Across three architecture families (Qwen GDN-MoE 35B/122B and a Mamba2-hybrid NoPE 120B) at 1M context on a single GPU in SGLang, windowing cuts the per-decode-step cost over the shipping native MTP draft by +28% to +44%, an input-invariant margin that widens with context. Since per-token latency is this cost divided by acceptance length, at matched acceptance end-to-end decode latency improves by the same amount, and more where windowing also lifts acceptance, while preserving the target's verified output distribution. Finally, the unread draft KV -- 7.7-11% of total KV at 1M -- is reclaimed via a compact ring buffer at no acceptance or quality cost.

</details>


### [122] [Zero-Flow Two-Sample Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21542)
*Yakun Wang,Leyang Wang,Song Liu,Taiji Suzuki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于零流准则的双样本检验新方法（ZF2ST），通过学习样本局部错位的有向模式来检测分布差异，在保持有效统计校准的同时实现了灵活的神经网络应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双样本检验方法在使用复杂模型（如神经网络）时难以同时保持有效的统计校准和强大的检验功效，需要一种能够灵活利用神经网络学习能力同时确保统计检验有效性的新框架。

Method: 提出基于零流准则的统计差异度量（ZFD），通过学习两分布样本间的局部错位有向模式作为分布差异的证据，将见证函数学习与假设检验评估分离，开发了基于回归和功效最大化两种见证函数学习方法，并使用神经网络实现。

Result: 在合成数据集和图像数据集上的实验表明，ZF2ST方法在检测结构化分布变化时能够实现较强的检验功效，同时能够保持良好校准的第一类错误率。

Conclusion: 所提出的零流双样本检验方法成功地将灵活的神经网络学习能力与有效的统计校准相结合，为双样本检验问题提供了一个统计有效且实际可行的解决方案，特别适用于检测结构化的分布差异。

Abstract: We propose a new approach to two-sample testing for deciding whether two sets of samples are drawn from the same distribution. The test is built on a statistical discrepancy based on the zero-flow criterion, termed zero-flow discrepancy (ZFD). We prove the validity of ZFD and propose a practical testing procedure, termed the zero-flow two-sample test (ZF2ST). The key idea is to learn how samples from the two distributions are locally misaligned and use the resulting directional pattern as evidence of distributional difference. By separating witness learning from hypothesis evaluation, ZF2ST can use flexible neural networks while maintaining valid statistical calibration. We develop both regression-based and power-maximized approaches for learning the witness. Experiments on synthetic and image datasets demonstrate that ZF2ST can achieve strong testing power for structured distributional changes while maintaining well-calibrated type-I error.

</details>


### [123] [X$^3$-OPD: Distilling Reasoning into Large Audio-Language Models via On-Policy Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21550)
*Dongjie Fu,Di Cao,Xize Cheng,Zihan Zhang,Wenxu Jia,Yifu Chen,Shengpeng Ji,Yu Zhang,Tao Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出X$^3$-OPD跨模态策略蒸馏框架，通过文本教师模型指导音频-语言学生模型，并构建三层对称语料库，显著提升音频语言模型的深度推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模音频-语言模型在听觉感知方面取得显著进展，但在深度逻辑推理方面仍落后于基于文本的大语言模型，主要原因是高质量音频推理数据的稀缺。

Method: 提出X$^3$-OPD跨模态策略蒸馏框架：(1) 学生模型基于自身声学感知生成推理轨迹，教师模型利用匹配的文本输入和验证答案提供token级指导；(2) 构建三层对称语料库，涵盖文本推理转语音、复杂声学场景的音频事件推理、以及包含副语言线索的口语对话推理，将跨模态蒸馏从文本可恢复内容扩展到非语言事件、韵律和对话上下文的推理。

Result: 在MMSU、MMAU、BIG Bench Audio和MMAR四个基准上的实验表明，X$^3$-OPD显著提升了音频锚定的推理能力和思维链质量，同时在域转移下基本保留了模型的现有能力。

Conclusion: X$^3$-OPD成功将文本模型的推理能力迁移至音频-语言模型，通过分层对称语料设计实现了从纯文本推理到非语言事件和韵律推理的拓展，为音频语言模型的深度推理提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: While large audio-language models have achieved remarkable progress in auditory perception, they still lag behind text-based large language models in deep logical reasoning, primarily due to the scarcity of high-quality audio reasoning data. To bridge this gap, we propose X$^3$-OPD, a cross-modal on-policy distillation framework that transfers reasoning capabilities from a powerful text teacher to an audio-language student. During training, the student generates reasoning trajectories conditioned on its own acoustic perception, while the teacher provides token-level guidance using matched textual inputs and verified answers. We further construct a three-tier symmetric corpus covering textual reasoning rendered into speech, audio-event reasoning grounded in complex acoustic scenes, and spoken-dialogue reasoning involving paralinguistic cues. This design extends cross-modal distillation beyond textually recoverable content to reasoning grounded in non-linguistic events, prosody, and conversational context. Experiments on MMSU, MMAU, BIG Bench Audio, and MMAR demonstrate that X$^3$-OPD substantially improves audio-grounded reasoning and chain-of-thought quality while largely preserving the model's existing capabilities under domain shift.

</details>


### [124] [Graph Learning on Ensembles of Cyclic Peptides: An Investigation of Molecular Ensemble Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21561)
*Aaron Feller,Kris Deibler,Maxim Secor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了EnsembleEGNN，一种编码构象集合的分子基础模型。预训练模型在环肽性质预测任务上显著优于从头训练和序列基线，表明构象集合编码能有效提升预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子性质预测方法通常使用单一代表性构象，但许多分子在溶液中以构象集合形式存在，单一构象无法充分表征分子的热力学性质。

Method: 提出EnsembleEGNN模型：1) 使用共享的等变图神经网络(EGNN)层编码每个构象子；2) 通过集合注意力块(Set Attention Block)池化构象子表示。在CREMP环肽集合数据集上进行多任务自监督预训练，包括掩码标记恢复、噪声坐标重建和成对距离重建。

Result: 在CREMP-CycPeptMPDB数据集上：从头训练完全失败(R²=0.005)；预训练模型达到R²=0.477和Pearson r=0.699，优于序列BERT基线(R²=0.439，r=0.667)；与BERT序列编码器端到端协同训练的混合模型进一步提升至R²=0.538和r=0.737。

Conclusion: 将构象集合编码为单一的热力学信息嵌入能够有效改进环肽性质预测，验证了构象集合表示相对于单一构象表示的优势。

Abstract: Molecular property prediction from structure often uses a single representative conformation, even though many molecules exist as conformational ensembles in solution. We introduce EnsembleEGNN, a molecular ensemble foundation model that encodes an ensemble by first encoding each conformer with shared Equivariant Graph Neural Network (EGNN) layers, then pooling the resulting conformer representations with a Set Attention Block. We pretrain the model on CREMP, a cyclic peptide ensemble dataset, using a multi-task self-supervised objective combining masked token recovery, noisy-coordinate reconstruction, and pairwise distance reconstruction. On the CREMP-CycPeptMPDB dataset, training EnsembleEGNN from scratch fails entirely ($R^2=0.005$). However, the pretrained model reaches $R^2=0.477$ and Pearson $r=0.699$, outperforming the sequence-only BERT baseline ($R^2=0.439$, Pearson $r=0.667$). When EnsembleEGNN is co-trained end-to-end with the BERT sequence encoder, the hybrid model improves further to $R^2=0.538$ and Pearson $r=0.737$. These results demonstrate that encoding conformational ensembles into a single thermodynamically informed embedding improves cyclic-peptide property prediction.

</details>


### [125] [Beyond Sufficiency: Time Series Explanation with Counterfactual Necessity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21573)
*Hongnan Ma,Yiwei Shi,Mengyue Yang,Weiru Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TimePNS框架，通过引入必要性意识来解决时间序列分类器解释中的虚假子序列问题，能更准确地识别决策关键子序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的面向充分性的时间序列解释方法会将高重要性分配给虚假子序列（支持预测但对模型决策非必需），导致解释不够准确。需要一种既能保持预测又能识别必要子序列的解释方法。

Method: 提出TimePNS必要性感知框架，采用两阶段设计：第一阶段学习可识别的因果生成过程和充分性解释掩码；第二阶段基于Pearl反事实必要性概念，对时间因子进行干预以推导必要性信号，通过时间门控机制抑制非必要成分并强调反事实必要成分。

Result: 在合成和真实时间序列基准测试上的实验表明，TimePNS能更准确地识别决策关键子序列，在充分性-必要性权衡方面始终优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: TimePNS成功解决了现有方法将高重要性分配给虚假子序列的问题，通过引入必要性意识实现了更准确的时间序列解释，为时间序列分类器的可解释性提供了新思路。

Abstract: Faithful explanations of time-series classifiers should identify subsequences that are not only sufficient to preserve a black-box model's prediction, but also necessary for maintaining it. However, existing sufficiency-oriented methods can assign high importance to spurious subsequences that support the prediction without being essential to the model's decision. We introduce \textbf{TimePNS}, a necessity-aware framework for time-series explanation. Inspired by Pearl's counterfactual notion of necessity, TimePNS assesses whether a temporal factor is necessary by intervening on it and measuring whether the original prediction is disrupted. The framework adopts a two-stage design. Stage I learns an identifiable causal generative process together with a sufficiency-oriented explanation mask. Stage II performs counterfactual interventions on temporal factors to derive necessity signals, which supervise a temporal gate that refines the initial explanation by suppressing non-essential components and emphasizing counterfactually necessary ones. Experiments on synthetic and real-world time-series benchmarks show that TimePNS more accurately identifies decision-critical subsequences and consistently improves sufficiency-necessity trade-offs over strong baselines.

</details>


### [126] [Expanding Flow Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21585)
*Sophia Tang,Pranam Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了EFlows和EFMs，这是一种新颖的流生成模型框架，能够处理动态变化维度和序列长度的生成任务。通过将扩张操作与传输映射解耦，实现了输出尺寸作为可学习自由度的目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于流的生成模型被限制在固定维度或固定序列长度的设定中，无法处理输出尺寸本身需要作为可学习自由度的场景。这一限制阻碍了流模型在需要动态调整输出规模的任务中的应用，如变长序列生成和变规模图生成等。

Method: 提出了Expanding Generative Flows (EFlows)框架，通过沿扩张插值路径在分布间建立流，并利用条件噪声逐步扩张状态空间。在此基础上构建了Expanding Flow Maps (EFMs)，将任意两个时间步之间的映射分解为两个可学习操作：(1) 扩张算子，基于当前状态用新坐标或词元扩张状态空间；(2) 传输映射，沿插值路径推进扩张状态。这些算子的组合形成同时执行扩张与去噪的单一映射。同时将框架扩展至离散单纯形以支持离散模态。

Result: 在连续和离散模态上都建立了理论框架。当扩张算子取恒等映射时，EFMs退化为现有的固定画布流模型，表明该框架是已有方法的推广。在离散设定中实现了变规模图生成和变长序列生成，证明了输出尺寸可作为可学习、可控制的自由度。

Conclusion: EFlows和EFMs为输出尺寸需要动态确定的生成任务提供了原则性框架，成功突破了流生成模型固定维度和固定序列长度的限制，为可变尺寸生成任务开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Flow-based generative models have enabled remarkable progress in fast and controllable generation across continuous and discrete state spaces, yet existing parameterizations are constrained to fixed dimensions or fixed sequence lengths. Here, we introduce Expanding Generative Flows (EFlows), which define flows between distributions of increasing dimensionality along an expanding interpolant that grows the state by augmenting it with conditional noise. Building on this construction, we propose Expanding Flow Maps (EFMs), a new class of flow maps that distill the expanding interpolant into efficient few-step generative models. Each EFM factors the map between any two timesteps into two learnable operations: an expand operator, which augments the state space with new coordinates or tokens conditioned on the current state, and a transport map, which pushes the expanded state forward along the interpolant. Composing these operators yields a single map that jointly expands and denoises the state, recovering existing fixed-canvas flows and flow maps as the special case in which the expand operator is the identity. We further extend the framework to the discrete simplex, enabling variable-size graph generation and variable-length sequence generation. Across both continuous and discrete modalities, we establish EFlows and EFMs as a principled framework for settings in which output size is itself a learned, controllable degree of freedom.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [WaveformQA: Benchmarking LLM Temporal Reasoning on Digital Waveforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20638)
*Yichuan Liu,Daniel Cummings,Nick Vadlamudi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了WaveformQA，首个用于评估大语言模型在数字波形时序推理能力的开源问答基准，包含360个多难度问题。实验表明LLM在复杂时序推理上表现不佳，但事件-时间JSON表示法比标准VCD格式能显著提升模型推理准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型在代码生成和推理方面表现出色，但其在数字波形数据上的时序推理能力仍未被探索。现有的基准主要评估硬件描述语言（HDL）代码生成，波形仅作为补充上下文，而波形推理是设计验证中的关键瓶颈。

Method: 提出了WaveformQA基准，包含360个通过程序生成的、具有真实答案的问答，分为八个难度类别，涵盖多信号相关性和事件排序等复杂问题。波形源自开源设计实现以确保可复现性和真实性。对比了标准VCD格式与事件-时间JSON表示法对模型推理的影响。

Result: 前沿LLM在简单查询上准确率合理，但在涉及复杂时序和多步骤问题时，因上下文窗口限制和推理困难导致性能下降。采用事件-时间JSON表示波形比标准VCD格式能提升LLM的推理准确率。

Conclusion: WaveformQA填补了LLM时序推理评估的空白，揭示了现有模型在复杂波形推理上的局限性，并为未来研究提供了可扩展的开源框架，支持新问题类别和波形源的导入。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in code generation and reasoning, yet their ability to perform temporal reasoning over digital waveform data remains largely unexplored. Although reasoning over digital waveforms is a critical bottleneck in design verification, existing benchmarks primarily evaluate hardware description language (HDL) code generation and use waveforms only as supplementary context. This paper presents WaveformQA, an open-source question-answering benchmark for evaluating LLM temporal reasoning over digital waveforms. The benchmark comprises 360 questions with programmatically generated ground truths across eight categories of varying difficulty, including questions targeting multi-signal correlation and event ordering. Waveforms are generated from open-source design implementations, ensuring reproducibility and grounding the benchmark in real hardware behavior. Evaluation of frontier LLMs reveals that while models achieve reasonable accuracy on simple queries, performance degrades due to context window limitations and reasoning difficulties on complex temporal and multi-step questions. In addition, we show that an event-time JSON representation of waveforms improves LLM reasoning accuracy versus the standardized value change dump (VCD) format. The open-source framework supports extending to new question categories and importing new waveform sources, enabling researchers to rapidly prototype temporal reasoning experiments.

</details>


### [128] [AINTMA: Agentic AI Architecture for Autonomous Test Management with Generative Intelligence, Secure Cloud Communication and Adaptive Quality Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20452)
*Vinil Pasupuleti,Shyalendar Reddy Allala,Siva Rama Krishna Varma Bayyavarapu,Shrey Tyagi,Srinivasateja Songa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AINTMA多智能体AI系统，通过六个专用AI代理协同工作，将传统测试管理转变为自主质量智能生态系统，在12个项目中实现88.4%测试优先级准确率、43%测试周期时间缩短、缺陷逃逸率从8.3%降至2.1%、340%投资回报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代软件质量保障需要能够在分布式云环境中进行自适应决策的智能自主系统，传统测试管理方法难以满足云规模企业环境下的质量管控需求，亟需将传统测试管理转型为自主的质量智能生态系统。

Method: 提出AINTMA（代理式智能测试管理架构）多智能体AI系统，部署六个专用AI代理（测试发现、风险评估、强化学习优先级排序、执行编排、生成式质量智能、云安全监控），通过云原生微服务基础设施上的安全多智能体通信框架进行协调。生成式质量智能代理使用大语言模型生成质量叙述，强化学习优先级代理将测试选择建模为马尔可夫决策过程，采用零信任API网关和OAuth2/JWT认证确保安全通信。

Result: 在12个异构软件项目18个月的评估中：测试优先级准确率达88.4% APFD（随机基线51.2%，最佳商业基线82.1%）；测试周期时间缩短43%；缺陷逃逸率从8.3%降至2.1%；9个月回本实现340% ROI；系统可扩展至50,000+测试用例，响应时间低于400ms；生成式智能模块获得4.3/5.0的开发者有用性评分。

Conclusion: 结合自主多智能体协调、生成式智能和安全智能连接的代理式AI能够根本性地推进云规模企业环境中的软件质量管理，为软件质量保障领域提供了一种可行的智能自主系统解决方案。

Abstract: Modern software quality assurance demands intelligent, autonomous systems capable of adaptive decision-making across distributed cloud environments. This paper presents AINTMA (Agentic Intelligent Test Management Architecture), a multi-agent agentic AI system that transforms traditional test management into an autonomous quality intelligence ecosystem. AINTMA deploys six specialized AI agents (Test Discovery, Risk Assessment, Reinforcement Learning Prioritization, Execution Orchestration, Generative Quality Intelligence, and Cloud Security Monitor) coordinated through a secure multi-agent communication framework over a cloud-native microservices infrastructure. The Generative Quality Intelligence agent employs large language models to produce plain language quality narratives, defect risk summaries, and data-augmented test recommendations. The RL Prioritization agent models test selection as a Markov Decision Process, learning contextual policies from large-scale historical test execution data (47 features, rolling 36-month window). Secure cloud communication is enforced through a zero-trust API gateway with OAuth2/JWT authentication, encrypted inter-agent messaging, and multi-tenant isolation. Evaluation across 12 heterogeneous software projects over 18 months demonstrates: 88.4% test prioritization accuracy (APFD, vs. 51.2% random, 82.1% best commercial baseline); 43% test cycle time reduction; defect escape rate reduced from 8.3% to 2.1%; 340% ROI at 9-month payback. The agentic architecture scales to 50,000+ test cases with sub-400ms response time, and the generative intelligence module achieves 4.3/5.0 developer usefulness rating. AINTMA demonstrates that agentic AI, combining autonomous multi-agent coordination, generative intelligence and secure smart connectivity, can fundamentally advance software quality management in cloud-scale enterprise environments.

</details>


### [129] [Marking the Wrong Symptoms: Evaluating LLM Watermarks in Medical Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20462)
*Melanie Rieff,Robin Staab,Thibaud Gloaguen,Stefan Hegselmann,Martin Vechev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性研究了LLM水印技术对医疗性能的影响，发现水印会导致医疗文本质量显著下降，现有评估指标无法有效检测这些临床相关的失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型正越来越多地集成到临床工作流程中，但大多数水印评估仅针对通用基准，忽略了医疗领域。在医疗领域，微小的词元级扰动可能导致显著的语义变化，因此迫切需要研究水印技术对医疗任务的影响。

Method: 研究团队在多种医疗任务上系统评估了5种水印方案，涵盖11个LLM和7个VLM模型。研究引入了经人类专家验证的审计流程，系统性地评估医疗推理质量、术语精确性以及幻觉现象，涵盖了单模态和多模态临床推理任务。

Result: 研究发现水印技术会导致多种失效模式下的性能显著下降，包括词汇损坏、幻觉术语、图像发现错误归因或遗漏。缺乏领域特异性分析和使用聚合指标会系统性地掩盖水印导致的实际性能下降。

Conclusion: 领域特异性评估是安全部署水印医疗模型的必要前提。现有基准测试可能会掩盖具有临床意义的失效，强调了在医疗应用中需要专门的评估方法来确保水印模型的安全性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into clinical workflows, stressing the need for reliable traceability of model-generated output with watermarking. Yet, most watermarks are evaluated on general-purpose benchmarks, leaving domains like medicine, where small token-level perturbations can result in significant semantic changes, underexplored. In this work, we present the first rigorous study of how LLM watermarks affect medical performance, benchmarking 5 watermarking schemes across 11 LLMs and 7 VLMs on various tasks spanning unimodal and multimodal clinical reasoning. Importantly, we complement existing evaluations by introducing a human-expert-validated pipeline for systematically auditing medical reasoning quality, terminological precision, and induced hallucinations. Our results reveal that watermarking can induce substantial degradation across multiple failure modes, including lexical corruption, hallucinated terminology, and amplified misattribution or omission of image findings. Notably, we find that the absence of domain-specific analyses, combined with aggregate metrics that miss failures inherent to clinical text, can systematically obscure practical watermark-induced degradations. Our findings establish domain-specific evaluation as a prerequisite for the safe deployment of watermarked models in medicine, where current benchmarks can otherwise mask clinically consequential failures.

</details>


### [130] [Workload-Aware Caching for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20495)
*Anas Mohamed,Kaizan Haque,Azal Ahmad Khan,Chetan Sharma,Shuwen Ge,Ali Anwar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向多智能体系统的工作负载感知缓存驱逐策略，结合重计算成本、DAG依赖数和智能体调用频率三种信号，在有限内存约束下有效降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统将复杂任务分解为DAG结构执行，天然存在跨查询缓存中间结果的机会。然而现有缓存驱逐策略仅基于访问历史统一处理所有缓存项，忽略了智能体执行环境中独有的结构和工作负载信号，导致缓存效率低下。

Method: 提出工作负载感知驱逐策略，将重计算成本、DAG依赖计数和智能体调用频率三种信号融合为统一评分函数，在内存约束下保留最有价值的缓存条目。

Result: 在三个多智能体基准测试上评估，相对无缓存基线延迟降低高达64.7%，相比次优有限容量基线平均降低31.1%，性能接近无限缓存，同时保持与竞争方法相当或更优的准确率。此外证明该方法与计划级缓存和并行智能体执行等优化技术互补。

Conclusion: 工作负载感知内容缓存能够有效利用智能体执行环境特有的结构和工作负载信息，显著提升多智能体系统效率，且与其他优化方法协同作用，共同解决多智能体流水线中的效率瓶颈。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems decompose complex tasks into directed acyclic graphs (DAGs) of specialized agent executions, creating natural opportunities for caching intermediate results across queries. However, existing cache eviction policies treat all cached entries uniformly based on access history, ignoring structural and workload signals uniquely available in agentic execution environments. We present a workload-aware eviction policy that combines three signals, namely recomputation cost, DAG dependency count, and agent invocation frequency, into a unified scoring function that retains the most valuable entries under memory constraints. Evaluated across three multi-agent benchmarks spanning diverse reuse regimes, our policy reduces latency by up to 64.7% relative to the uncached baseline and achieves on average a 31.1% latency reduction over the next best finite-capacity baseline, while approaching the performance of an unbounded cache and maintaining accuracy on par with or exceeding all competing finite-capacity methods. We further show that workload-aware content caching is complementary to other agentic system optimization methods, including plan-level caching and parallel agent execution, with each technique targeting a distinct efficiency bottleneck in multi-agent pipelines.

</details>


### [131] [ClickGuard: Detecting and Spoiling Clickbait News with Informativeness Measures and Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20463)
*Wojciech Michaluk,Tymoteusz Urban,Mateusz Kubita,Soveatin Kuntur,Anna Wróblewska*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个AI驱动的浏览器扩展，通过混合机器学习架构识别点击诱饵文章，F1分数达91%，并提供预警、解释和文章摘要功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帮助用户识别和避免互联网上的误导性点击诱饵文章，超越传统的检测方法，提供更全面的用户保护机制。

Method: 采用混合机器学习架构，结合基于Transformer的嵌入表示、语言学动机特征以及自定义的"诱饵度"评分；评估了从经典向量化方法到大语言模型嵌入等多种NLP技术，最终开发了基于XGBoost的模型。

Result: 在开放组合数据集上达到91%的F1分数；浏览器扩展能够在用户访问点击诱饵文章前后发出警告，提供表示点击诱饵可能性的百分比分数，并基于分析指标解释预测结果；还提供点击诱饵摘要功能（一到两句话的文章总结）。

Conclusion: 成功开发了一款实用的浏览器扩展工具，不仅能高效检测点击诱饵，还能为用户提供预警、可解释的预测结果和文章摘要，有效帮助用户避免误导性内容。

Abstract: This paper presents an AI-driven browser extension that identifies clickbait to help users avoid misleading Internet articles. Moving beyond traditional detection, the application employs a hybrid machine learning architecture that combines transformer-based embeddings with linguistically motivated features and a custom "baitness" score. After evaluating various natural language processing techniques -- from classic vectorizers to large language model (LLM) embeddings -- an XGBoost-based model was developed that achieves an F1-score of 91% on the open combined dataset. Most importantly, the tool can warn users before and after they access a clickbait article. After opening an article, the user receives a percentage score indicating the likelihood that it is clickbait. The prediction is explained based on the analyzed metrics, including those specifically developed within the proposed system. The browser extension also provides a clickbait spoiler -- a one- to two-sentence summary of the entire article. Demo video:https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=IJ1gkQV82C4}{https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=IJ1gkQV82C4

</details>


### [132] [MKEvolve: A Modular Multi-Agent Framework for Kernel Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20501)
*Jason Yoo,Rajarshi Saha,Shaowei Zhu,Tao Yu,Wei Tang,Youngsuk Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MKEvolve是一个模块化内核演化框架，通过迭代分解PyTorch模块并协同优化LLM生成的子内核，显著提升了硬件加速器内核的正确性和性能，同时降低了令牌使用量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于LLM的代码生成取得了快速进展，但为硬件加速器编写正确且高性能的内核仍然是扩展现代机器学习工作负载的关键瓶颈。现有的端到端直接合成方法在处理复杂模块时存在正确性和性能不足的问题。

Method: 提出MKEvolve（模块化内核演化）框架，采用迭代协同演化方法：1）将复杂PyTorch模块进行模块化分解；2）对每个子模块使用LLM生成内核；3）通过跨迭代的拆分和融合操作优化分解结构；4）对每个子内核进行独立的LLM驱动的束搜索优化。最终生成可独立验证的子内核的程序化组合。

Result: 在KernelBench L2和L3级别（涵盖多算子序列和完整模型架构）的Triton实验中，MKEvolve在正确性和加速比上均优于端到端直接合成基线方法，同时将LLM令牌使用量减少了最多35%。生成的内核具有可配置性（子内核实现可替换）、可解释性（错误和加速可追溯到特定子内核）和良好的适应性。

Conclusion: MKEvolve通过模块化分解和协同演化策略有效解决了硬件加速器内核生成的关键挑战，在提升正确性和性能的同时显著降低了计算成本，为可扩展的机器学习工作负载提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Despite rapid progress in LLM-based code generation, writing correct and performant kernels for hardware accelerators remains a key bottleneck in scaling modern ML workloads. We present MKEvolve (Modular Kernel Evolve), a framework that iteratively co-evolves a modular decomposition of complex PyTorch modules and the LLM-generated kernel for each submodule, refining the decomposition by splitting and fusing across iterations while independently improving each subkernel via LLM-driven beam search. The resulting kernels are programmatic compositions of independently verified subkernels, making them configurable (subkernel implementations are swappable), interpretable (errors and speedups are traceable to specific subkernels), and readily adaptable to related model architectures. Experiments with Triton on KernelBench L2 and L3, spanning multi-operator sequences and full model architectures, show that MKEvolve improves both correctness and speedup over end-to-end direct synthesis baselines while reducing LLM token usage by up to 35%.

</details>


### [133] [Stochastic Sampling is Epistemically Shallow: The Dimensionality Gap Between Temperature Variation and Model Diversity in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20464)
*Izhar Ali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 单模型多次采样（自一致性）仅能提供问题级不确定性估计，无法揭示跨问题结构；只有多样化集成才能揭示模型未知内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究单模型在不同运行间的变化是否像集成模型一样能揭示跨问题的结构信息，即相关问题的答案是否会协同翻转，从而反映模型的知识盲区。

Method: 对比两种设置在相同问题集上的表现：（1）单个模型在温度τ=1下运行100次；（2）24个LLM组成的集成在τ=0下各运行一次。使用Marchenko-Pastur随机矩阵检验区分信号与采样噪声。在五个模型家族和三个基准（MMLU、HellaSwag、GSM8K）上进行实验。

Result: 单模型多次采样中，最多只有一个维度超过噪声水平；而集成模型有四个特征值超出噪声边界，且匹配难度的伯努利零分布在500次蒙特卡洛模拟中至多产生一个超过噪声的特征值。自一致性方法虽能准确估计问题级不确定性，但未检测到跨问题结构。

Conclusion: 单模型的自一致性变化只能提供问题级不确定性估计，无法揭示跨问题的潜在结构；只有多样化的集成模型才能有效揭示模型未知的知识结构。

Abstract: When a language model gives different answers on repeated runs, does that variation reveal what it does not know? Self-consistency turns the variation into a per-question uncertainty estimate via majority voting. But does the same variation reveal cross-question structure -- related questions flipping together, the way a diverse ensemble does? We compare two regimes on the same questions: one model run $100$ times at $τ=1$ versus an ensemble of $24$ LLMs run once each at $τ=0$. A Marchenko--Pastur random-matrix test separates signal from sampling noise on both sides. Within any single model, at most one dimension rises above noise across five families and three benchmarks (MMLU, HellaSwag, GSM8K). Across the ensemble, four eigenvalues clear the noise edge, while a matched-difficulty Bernoulli null produces at most one in $500$ Monte Carlo draws. Self-consistency gives accurate per-question uncertainty but no detectable cross-question structure; only a diverse ensemble surfaces what a model does not know.

</details>


### [134] [AppWorld-UL: Benchmarking Diverse Agent-User Interactions for Tool-Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20536)
*Junzhi Chen,Harsh Trivedi,Jane Pan,Michael JQ Zhang,Tejas Srinivasan,Niranjan Balasubramanian,Ashish Sabharwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了AppWorld-UL基准测试，用于评估需要与用户交互的工具使用智能体，当前最先进模型(Claude Opus 4.7)仅达到48.6%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的智能体-用户交互基准测试存在两大不足：一是无法捕捉交互的多样性，二是运行在小型环境中，API数量少且通常不改变状态。日常数字任务(如订购食品)中的智能体不仅需要操作应用程序，还需要与用户进行多种交互(如提出澄清问题、请求确认、告知指令不可行等)，因此需要更全面的评估基准。

Method: 基于AppWorld框架(包含Amazon、Spotify等9个流行模拟应用)，系统性地修改原始任务以引入歧义和约束条件，从而需要各种类型的智能体-用户交互。使用LLM模拟用户行为，通过精心设计的知识边界进行提示，比以往工作中的无约束或过度僵化的替代方案提供更可靠的模拟。构建了包含516个挑战性任务的"用户在环"基准测试。

Result: 最先进的LLM(Claude Opus 4.7)在AppWorld-UL上仅达到48.6%的成功率，在更难的组合任务子集上仅达到35.7%。在更严格的场景级指标上，组合任务性能下降至仅21.3%。分析表明正确的用户交互对成功至关重要。

Conclusion: 该基准测试展示了显著的难度，证明了其在推进用户在环的工具使用智能体研究方面的潜力。研究揭示了正确进行用户交互对任务成功的关键作用，为未来研究提供了重要方向。

Abstract: Tool-use agents that address day-to-day digital tasks such as ordering groceries must not only operate applications, but also interact with the user, e.g., to ask clarification questions, prompt for confirmation, and inform the user when the instruction is infeasible. However, current benchmarks for evaluating agent-user interactions do not capture the diversity of such interactions. Further, they operate in small environments with few, often non-state-changing, APIs. To address this gap, we introduce AppWorld-UL, a ``user-in-the-loop'' benchmark of 516 challenging tasks requiring diverse agent-user interactions. Building upon the AppWorld framework with 9 popular simulated apps like Amazon and Spotify, we systematically modify original tasks to introduce ambiguities and constraints that necessitate various types of agent-user interaction. User behavior is simulated by an LLM prompted to respond with carefully designed knowledge boundaries, offering more reliable simulation than the unconstrained or overly rigid alternatives used in prior work. Our evaluation reveals that a state-of-the-art LLM, Claude Opus 4.7, achieves only 48.6% success on AppWorld-UL, and only 35.7% on the harder, compositional subset. On the stricter, scenario-level metric, compositional task performance drops to only 21.3%. Our analysis reveals that correct user-interaction is crucial for success. This demonstrates the benchmark's difficulty and its potential to advance research on user-in-the-loop tool-use agents.

</details>


### [135] [JAXBench: Benchmarking Autonomous TPU Kernel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20466)
*Arya Tschand,Charles Hong,Julian Walker,Nina Cai,Shangkun Wang,Suvinay Subramanian,Sundar Dev,Vijay Janapa Reddi,Amir Yazdanbakhsh,Sethu Sankaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了JAXBench，这是首个TPU原生基准测试套件，用于AI生成的内核优化，包含50个JAX工作负载，在Gemini 3 Flash上实现了1.36倍的平均加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管严格的基准测试推动了GPU内核性能优化的进展，但TPU领域缺乏等效的共享优化目标基准，这阻碍了TPU上AI生成内核优化的研究进展。

Method: 创建了包含50个JAX工作负载的JAXBench基准测试套件：从MaxText库（包括Llama-3.1、DeepSeek-V3、Mixtral、Mamba-2和AlphaFold2）提取17个生产ML算子，从KernelBench移植33个算子，其中8个算子附带Tokamax库的手工优化Pallas内核作为专家上界基线。在Gemini 3 Flash上评估了四种反馈驱动的Pallas内核生成方法。

Result: 针对Pallas这类文档稀疏的DSL，特定目标的上下文比模型规模更重要：基于TPU文档的条件下正确率从5.8%提升至37.3%，并在50个基准中解决48个，达到1.28倍平均加速。Autocomp的束搜索管道相比XLA实现1.36倍平均加速；在8个手工调优内核上达到1.60倍平均加速，恢复了2.08倍Tokamax上界的绝大部分性能。

Conclusion: 高质量的TPU内核优化仍然是一项具有挑战性的任务，论文开源了JAXBench基准、评估工具和基线结果以支持开源贡献。

Abstract: Rigorous benchmarks have driven progress in autonomous GPU kernel performance optimization by establishing a shared target to hillclimb on, but no equivalent exists for TPUs. We present JAXBench, a TPU-native benchmark suite for AI-generated kernel optimization on Google Cloud TPUs. JAXBench comprises 50 JAX workloads that are both relevant and provide headroom for optimization. We extract 17 production ML operators from architectures in the public MaxText library such as Llama-3.1, DeepSeek-V3, Mixtral, Mamba-2, and AlphaFold2, and translate 33 operators from KernelBench that are validated for correctness and set with new problem sizes that achieve high TPU v6e MXU utilization. Eight of the 17 production operators ship with hand-optimized Pallas kernels from the public Tokamax library and block-size tuned to establish an expert upper-bound baseline. We evaluate four feedback-driven methods on generating candidate Pallas kernels for JAXBench. Across the full suite with Gemini 3 Flash, we find that target-specific context matters more than model scale on a sparsely-documented DSL like Pallas. Conditioning on curated TPU documentation raises per-sample correctness from 5.8% to 37.3% and solves 48 of 50 benchmarks at a 1.28x geomean speedup. Search structure yields significant gains once correctness is achieved, with Autocomp's beam-search pipeline reaching a 1.36x geomean speedup over XLA. On the 8 hand-tuned kernels, Autocomp reaches 1.60x geomean over XLA, recovering most of the 2.08x Tokamax upper bound but trailing on the specialized paged and ragged attention operators. High-quality TPU kernel optimization remains a challenging task, and we release the JAXBench benchmark, evaluation harness, and baseline results to support open source contributions.

</details>


### [136] [DC-Leap: Training-Free Acceleration of dLLMs via Draft-Guided Contiguous Leaping Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20467)
*Yanhua Jiao,Tianyi Wu,Xiaoxi Sun,Yulin Li,HuiLing Zhen,Libo Qin,Baotian Hu,Zhuotao Tian,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DC-Leap框架，通过动态连续验证策略解决并行解码中的联合概率依赖错误，在中等置信度下实现扩散大语言模型的可靠加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前扩散大语言模型的并行解码策略受到过于保守的置信度阈值限制，这些阈值由联合概率依赖错误（JPDE）所导致，造成冗余去噪迭代和次优推理速度。

Method: 提出DC-Leap训练无关框架，包含动态连续验证策略（将严格有序因果约束集成到并行解码过程中）和草稿引导解码机制（通过多token跳跃扩展上下文），有效消除JPDE。

Result: 在标准基准测试上，MBPP长序列生成实现高达53.19倍加速，结合KV-Cache时实现高达105.02倍加速，同时保持相当的生成质量。

Conclusion: DC-Leap成功在中等置信度领域实现dLLMs的可靠加速，在不牺牲生成质量的前提下显著提升了推理效率。

Abstract: While parallel decoding is central to the efficiency of Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs), current strategies are often hindered by overly conservative confidence thresholds. These thresholds, necessitated by the Joint Probability Dependence Error (JPDE), result in redundant denoising iterations and suboptimal inference speeds. To overcome this, we propose DC-Leap, a training-free framework that enables reliable acceleration of dLLMs in the moderate-confidence regime. DC-Leap introduces a Dynamic Contiguous Verification strategy that integrates strictly-ordered causal constraints into the parallel decoding process. By progressively validating token dependencies, this mechanism effectively neutralizes the JPDE, enabling reliable acceleration with comparable performance. Furthermore, DC-Leap incorporates the draft-guided decoding mechanism, where the draft helps extend the context by leaping forward across multiple tokens, providing look-ahead context and retaining the structural benefits of bidirectional attention during inference. Extensive experiments on standard benchmarks demonstrate that DC-Leap achieves substantial speedups, up to 53.19x on MBPP for long-sequence generation, and up to 105.02x when combined with KV-Cache with comparable generation quality. Code is available at https://github.com/ffh-wyls/DC-Leap .

</details>


### [137] [HiMe: Real-Time Self-Hosted Personal Agent Platform for Health Insights with Wearable Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21019)
*Wei Liu,Siya Qi,Linhai Zhang,Lorainne Tudor Car,Yulan He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了HiMe，一个可本地部署、隐私优先的智能体平台，用于实时处理可穿戴设备健康数据，实现个性化健康监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统可穿戴健康信号分析方法受限于刚性分析框架和有限的个性化能力。虽然LLM智能体为个人健康智能体分析创造了新机遇，但目前缺乏开源、可本地部署且能实时处理个人健康数据同时保护隐私的平台。

Method: 提出HiMe平台，遵循三个设计原则：1）将数据库视为一等组件；2）联合优化效果与效率，实现低成本帕累托最优平衡；3）实时处理数据同时对用户进行长期建模。该平台兼容多种可穿戴设备的实时健康数据生态系统。

Result: 开发了一个完全兼容多种可穿戴设备实时健康数据生态系统的本地可部署平台，能够在保护隐私的前提下实现个人健康数据的实时处理。

Conclusion: 这些设计原则使得个人能够实际利用个人健康智能体进行连续、个性化的健康监测，从而改善健康福祉。

Abstract: Traditional approaches to wearable health signal analysis, such as smartwatches, are constrained by rigid analytical frameworks and limited personalisation. The emergence of LLM agents creates a new opportunity for Personal Health Agentic Analysis, where health insights can be generated adaptively and in context. However, currently there is no open-source locally deployable platform capable of processing personal health data in real time while preserving privacy. We present HiMe, a locally deployable, privacy-first agent platform that is fully compatible with real-time health data ecosystems across a wide range of wearable devices. HiMe is guided by three design principles. The database is treated as a first-class component. Effectiveness and efficiency are jointly optimised to achieve a low-cost Pareto-optimal balance. Data are processed in real time while the user is modelled over the long term. Together, these principles make it practical for individuals to harness Personal Health Agents for continuous, personalised health monitoring for better wellbeing.

</details>


### [138] [CRAWO: Custom Resources for Adaptive Workload Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20490)
*Eugênio Santos,Daniel Maia,Stefano Loss,José Manoel Silva,Aluizio Rocha Neto,Thais Batista,Everton Cavalcante,Nélio Cacho,Eduardo Nogueira,Daniel Araújo,Frederico Lopes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CRAWO框架，通过控制循环模型和硬件感知的资源分配器，实现了异构边缘环境下AI流水线的自适应工作负载编排，有效降低了延迟敏感场景对中心化云处理的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘智能通过将计算从中心化云数据中心迁移至网络边缘，能够减少延迟和带宽消耗，从而支持智慧城市的实时应用。然而，在异构边缘基础设施上部署AI流水线仍面临重大挑战，因为设备能力跨度巨大，从低功耗微控制器到配备加速器的系统不等。现有的边缘编排平台主要关注部署自动化和基础设施管理，但这些方法往往效率低下，限制了在动态条件下自适应分配资源的能力。

Method: 本文引入CRAWO（Custom Resources for Adaptive Workload Orchestration），一种用于在分布式边缘环境中协调AI流水线的架构框架。CRAWO采用基于控制循环的模型，通过管理放置决策、状态管理和阶段间数据流，同时在边缘节点上实例化服务，将分配智能与执行分离。框架包含一个硬件感知分配器和一个可插拔的多准则决策层，利用实时基础设施指标实现自适应工作负载放置。参考实现采用部署在轻量级Kubernetes发行版（K3s）上的微服务架构，使用自定义资源定义（CRD）进行领域建模，并使用专用操作器进行状态协调。

Result: 在车牌识别的车辆监控场景中进行的评估表明，CRAWO改善了工作负载分布，并减少了对延迟敏感环境中中心化云处理的依赖。

Conclusion: CRAWO框架能够有效解决异构边缘基础设施上AI流水线部署的挑战，通过自适应工作负载编排实现了更好的资源分配和更低的延迟依赖。

Abstract: Edge Intelligence has emerged as a key paradigm for enabling real-time applications in smart cities by shifting computation from centralized cloud data centers to the network edge, thereby reducing latency and bandwidth consumption. However, deploying Artificial Intelligence (AI) pipelines across heterogeneous edge infrastructures remains challenging due to the wide range of device capabilities, from low-power microcontrollers to accelerator-equipped systems. Existing edge orchestration platforms primarily focus on deployment automation and infrastructure management, but these approaches are often inefficient and limit the ability to adaptively allocate resources under dynamic conditions. To tackle these issues, this paper introduces CRAWO (Custom Resources for Adaptive Workload Orchestration), an architectural framework for coordinating AI pipelines across distributed edge environments. CRAWO follows a control-loop-based model that separates allocation intelligence from execution by managing placement decisions, state management, and inter-stage data flows while instantiating services on edge nodes. The framework incorporates a hardware-aware allocator with a pluggable multi-criteria decision layer that leverages real-time infrastructure metrics to enable adaptive workload placement. The reference implementation adopts a microservices architecture deployed on a lightweight Kubernetes distribution (K3s), using Custom Resource Definitions (CRDs) for domain modeling and a dedicated operator for state reconciliation. Evaluation in a vehicle surveillance scenario using license plate recognition demonstrates improved workload distribution and reduced reliance on centralized cloud processing in latency-sensitive environments.

</details>


### [139] [AI-Driven Multi-Hop Relay Selection for Smart Urban NR-V2X Networks via Learning-to-Optimize Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20554)
*Giambattista Amati,Federica Mangiatordi,Simone Angelini,Emiliano Pallotti,Pierpaolo Salvo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于图神经网络(GNN)的学习优化(L2O)框架，实现NR-V2X实时中继选择，在保持与MILP优化器相当连通性的同时，将执行时间降低数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集城市场景下的NR-V2X通信面临路侧单元(RSU)密度有限、频繁非视距条件和高度动态车联网拓扑等挑战，导致许多联网自动驾驶车辆(CAV)难以维持稳定的单跳连通性。虽然多跳中继辅助通信可扩展基础设施覆盖范围，但在实际流量、容量和连通性约束下实时选择中继链路仍具挑战性。混合整数线性规划(MILP)虽能产生最优多跳中继决策，但其计算复杂度随网络密度急剧增加，限制了实时应用性。

Method: 提出基于图神经网络(GNN)的学习优化(L2O)框架用于实时NR-V2X中继选择：1)将车辆通信状态建模为属性图，其中CAV和RSU作为节点，候选无线链路作为边并 enriched with 传播感知特征；2)采用离线MILP优化器提供最优监督；3)使用边感知图同构网络(GINE)近似优化器决策，实现近乎恒定的推理延迟；4)通过集成SUMO-GEMV2仿真流程生成大规模城市场景数据集进行验证。

Result: 在大规模城市场景数据集上的实验表明，所提方法实现了与MILP优化器相当的连通性性能，同时将执行时间降低了数个数量级，实现了近恒定推理延迟，有效支持可扩展、实时的NR-V2X运行。

Conclusion: 该框架通过利用现有车辆资产实现成本效益高的城市V2X连通性增强，支持智慧城市场景下的可扩展、实时NR-V2X运行，为智能移动应用提供可靠低延迟通信保障。

Abstract: Reliable and low-latency NR-V2X communications are essential for smart mobility in dense urban environments. However, limited Road-Side Unit (RSU) density, frequent non-line-of-sight conditions, and highly dynamic vehicular topologies often prevent many Connected and Automated Vehicles (CAVs) from maintaining stable single-hop connectivity. Although multi-hop relay-assisted communication can extend infrastructure coverage, selecting relay links in real time under practical flow, capacity, and connectivity constraints remains challenging.
  Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP) yields optimal multi-hop relay decisions, but its computational complexity scales sharply with network density, limiting real-time applicability. To address this, we propose a Learning-to-Optimise (L2O) framework based on Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) for real-time NR-V2X relay selection. Vehicular communication states are modeled as attributed graphs, where CAVs and RSUs are nodes and candidate radio links are enriched with propagation-aware features. An offline MILP oracle provides optimal supervision, while an edge-aware Graph Isomorphism Network (GINE) approximates oracle decisions with near-constant inference latency. Experiments on large-scale urban datasets generated by an integrated SUMO--GEMV2 simulation pipeline show that the proposed approach achieves connectivity comparable to that of the MILP oracle while reducing execution time by orders of magnitude. The framework enables cost-effective enhancement of urban V2X connectivity by leveraging existing vehicular assets and supporting scalable, real-time NR-V2X operation in smart city environments.

</details>


### [140] [InferenceBench: A Benchmark for Open-Ended LLM Inference Optimization by AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20468)
*Jehyeok Yeon,Ben Rank,Maksym Andriushchenko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了InferenceBench基准测试，用于评估AI代理在开放性AI工程任务中的表现。实验显示代理能够改进基线（最高8.08倍），但仍低于简单的超参数搜索（最高11.53倍）。主要瓶颈在于缺乏系统性探索和评估多样化配置的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试通常在预设工作流或狭窄动作空间上评估AI代理，即使是开放式任务也往往可以通过检索已知方案解决，难以判断结果是否反映真正的优化能力还是记忆的解决方案。需要一个新的基准测试来评估代理在开放性AI工程环境中的真实能力。

Method: 引入InferenceBench基准，要求代理部署OpenAI兼容推理服务器并优化LLM推理速度。每个代理获得目标LLM、一个H100 GPU、优化场景和两小时时间预算。设计了四个优化场景：预填充延迟、解码延迟、并发请求吞吐量以及三者平衡。测试了15个前沿代理配置。

Result: 代理相对朴素PyTorch基线可靠地提升（最高8.08倍），经常达到或超过默认设置的推理引擎（vLLM为4.05倍），但仍低于相同时间预算下的简单超参数搜索（最高11.53倍）。轨迹分析显示代理虽然列举多种优化技术，但过度收敛到单一推理框架，仅测试少数配置，大部分时间用于重新测量、修复或优化超参数。

Conclusion: InferenceBench反映了代理在开放性AI工程设置中的能力，记忆的解决方案只能带来有限改进。主要瓶颈不是领域知识，而是提出多样化配置、系统性评估并提交最佳解决方案的能力。这为未来改进AI代理的系统探索能力提供了重要研究方向。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly used to automate research and development tasks, yet existing benchmarks typically evaluate them on prescribed workflows or narrow action spaces. Even nominally open-ended tasks can often be solved by retrieving a well-known recipe and tuning a few hyperparameters, making it unclear whether strong results reflect genuine optimization or memorized solutions. We introduce InferenceBench, where an agent must deploy an OpenAI-compatible inference server and optimize the speed of LLM inference. Each agent receives a target LLM, one H100 GPU, an optimization scenario, and a wall-clock time budget of two hours. Three optimization scenarios isolate distinct bottlenecks of inference (prefill latency, decode latency, and concurrent request throughput) and a fourth balances all three at the same time. Across 15 frontier agent configurations, agents reliably improve over a naive PyTorch baseline (up to $8.08\times$) and often match or exceed serving engines with default settings ($4.05\times$ for vLLM), but still fall below a simple hyperparameter search under the same time budget (up to $11.53\times$). Qualitative analysis of agent trajectories shows that although agents enumerate many relevant optimization techniques, they overwhelmingly converge on a single inference framework. They test only a few distinct configurations and spend the remaining budget re-measuring, repairing, or optimizing hyperparameters rather than exploring substantially different strategies. This suggests the bottleneck is not domain knowledge, but the ability to propose diverse configurations, evaluate them systematically, and submit the best identified solution. Overall, InferenceBench reflects the ability of agents to operate in an open-ended AI engineering setting, where memorized solutions lead to limited improvements.

</details>


### [141] [SafeStep: AI-powered Travel Assistance for Elderly People with Frailty or Dementia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21156)
*Elderly People with Frailty or Dementia Azul Debenedetti,David Gamez,Franco Such,Nik Kairinos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SafeStep，一个AI驱动的旅行辅助系统，旨在帮助英国超过一百万的虚弱症或痴呆症患者解决城市出行困难。通过整合LLMs和Anticip8行为预测引擎，系统能够生成个性化失败场景、提出针对性干预措施，并提高用户出行的安全性和信心。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 英国有超过一百万人患有虚弱症或痴呆症，这些疾病严重影响了他们在城市环境中的出行能力。这一群体面临出行安全、导航困难等问题，亟需一种能够提供个性化支持并提高出行成功率的智能辅助系统。

Method: SafeStep系统的核心是一种新颖的出行图谱表示，将路径规划与预测建模相结合。对于旅程的每个阶段，系统通过以下步骤工作：(i) 结合大语言模型和Anticip8行为预测引擎生成个性化失败场景；(ii) 提出针对性的干预措施；(iii) 估算干预措施对结果概率的影响。基于此，系统能够选择最优干预措施以最大化用户到达目的地的可能性。

Result: 通过出行图谱生成实验和涉及26次真实出行的实地研究进行评估。结果表明，将Anticip8用于失败预测与基于GPT的模型用于干预评估相结合，能够获得最可靠的性能。用户反馈显示SafeStep提高了出行过程中的信心和感知安全性，但针对目标人群的界面可用性仍需改进。

Conclusion: SafeStep系统成功展示了AI在辅助老年人和弱势群体城市出行方面的潜力。未来计划改进并发布SafeStep，同时该系统开发的AI技术可应用于心理健康、职业指导和成瘾治疗等其他领域。

Abstract: More than a million people in the UK suffer from frailty or dementia, which severely compromise their ability to travel in urban environments. This paper presents SafeStep, an AI-driven travel system that assists elderly users with their journeys. At the core of SafeStep is a novel travel graph representation, which integrates route planning with predictive modelling. For each stage of a journey, the system (i) generates personalized failure scenarios using a combi-nation of LLMs and the Anticip8 behavioral prediction engine, (ii) proposes targeted interventions, and (iii) estimates the impact of interventions on out-come probabilities. This enables SafeStep to select interventions that maximize the likelihood of the person reaching their destination. SafeStep was evaluated through experiments on travel graph generation and a field study involving 26 real-world journeys. Results showed that combining Anticip8 for failure pre-diction with GPT-based models for intervention evaluation yields the most re-liable performance. User feedback indicated that SafeStep improves confidence and perceived safety during travel, although interface usability needs to be im-proved for the target demographic. In the future, we would like to improve and release SafeStep. The AI system that was developed for SafeStep could be ap-plied in other areas, such as mental health, career coaching and addiction treatment.

</details>


### [142] [DecodeShare: Tracing the Shared Subspace of LLM Decode-Time Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20469)
*Zishan Shao,Lixun Zhang,Kangning Cui,Yixiao Wang,Ting Jiang,Hancheng Ye,Qinsi Wang,Zhixu Du,Yuzhe Fu,Fan Yang,Danyang Zhuo,Yiran Chen,Hai Helen Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了DecodeShare协议，发现在KV缓存推理的解码阶段存在跨任务共享的低维子空间，该子空间对模型决策性能具有因果重要性，可用于改进激活引导的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在KV缓存推理场景下，预填充阶段和解码阶段的计算过程分离。现有研究尚不清楚在解码阶段是否存在跨任务共享的任务通用结构，以及这种结构（若存在）对模型功能有何因果作用。

Method: 提出DecodeShare协议：首先在解码时的隐藏状态中识别跨任务一致共享的低维子空间，然后通过消融实验（仅在该子空间上进行扰动）检验其因果作用；并与预填充阶段导出的子空间及随机子空间作对照。

Result: 在同等干预强度下，扰动所发现的解码共享子空间导致的决策性能下降远大于扰动预填充子空间或随机子空间；该子空间与常见的激活引导方向重叠，投影掉该子空间可分离两个成分的功能角色；在解码阶段评估引导向量比基于预填充的代理更可靠。

Conclusion: 解码阶段的隐藏状态中存在紧凑但关键的跨任务共享子空间，构成了高杠杆的因果通道，对激活引导和下游部署具有实践意义。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) handle many tasks with one set of parameters, but under KV-cached inference it is unclear what task-general structure, if any, is used at decode time rather than during prefill. We propose DecodeShare, a protocol that identifies a low-dimensional subspace consistently shared across tasks in decode-time hidden states, and then tests its causal role by removing that subspace only during decoding. In our experiments, disturbing the discovered shared subspace degrades decision performance far more than disturbing either a prefill-derived or random subspace under the same intervention budget. We further show this decode-shared subspace has practical consequences for activation steering: common steering directions can overlap the task-general decode channel. Projecting out this shared subspace directly separates the functional roles of the two components, while evaluating steering vectors at decode-time yields more reliable signal for downstream deployment than prefill-based proxies. Despite its compactness, the shared subspace can serve as a high-leverage causal channel at decode time. Code is available at: https://github.com/Zishan-Shao/decodeshare.git.

</details>


### [143] [Clustered Edge Intelligence: Beyond Just Convergence of Edge Computing and AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20937)
*Chinmaya Kumar Dehury,Boris Sedlak,Alaa Saleh,Ilir Murturi,Lauri Loven,Satish Narayana Srirama,Praveen Kumar Donta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“Clustered Edge Intelligence (CEI)”的前瞻性智能中心化框架，旨在将边缘计算中衍生的智能视为一等公民实体，实现跨异构边缘设备的智能描述、发现、观察、共享、重用和动态聚合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着从信息时代向智能时代的演进，数据的价值正逐渐让位于从中衍生的智能。然而，现有边缘智能研究主要聚焦于利用AI进行边缘资源管理或在边缘设备上部署轻量级AI模型，缺乏一个以智能为中心的框架，无法将衍生智能作为一等公民实体进行独立管理、共享和重用。

Method: 提出了Clustered Edge Intelligence (CEI)这一前瞻性的智能中心化方法，构建了一个三层CEI架构，并系统性地审视了实现该愿景所需的关键使能技术和研究维度，包括智能库存、语义知识表示、通信、可发现性、可观察性、生命周期自动化、聚类机制、市场机制、互操作性和标准化等。

Result: 通过提出CEI框架，为边缘计算领域提供了一个将智能作为可共享、可重用一等公民实体的愿景蓝图，并明确了其核心架构以及实现该框架所需的一系列使能技术栈和研究维度。

Conclusion: CEI框架旨在根本性改变当前边缘智能的研究范式，通过将智能实体化、标准化和市场化，实现边缘-云连续统中的智能高效流通与利用，为未来智能时代的边缘计算基础设施奠定理论基础。

Abstract: We are moving from an information age to the age of intelligence. A decade, or possibly less than that, data will not be the gold anymore rather the derived intelligence out of the data and the information we posses from the edge of the network. Existing Edge Intelligence research focuses mainly on two directions: using AI for edge resource management and deploying lightweight AI models on edge devices. However, existing edge computing research lacks an intelligence-centric framework in which derived intelligence is treated as a first-class, independently manageable entity that can be described, discovered, observed, shared, reused, and dynamically clustered across heterogeneous edge devices and applications. To address these research gaps, we introduced Clustered Edge Intelligence, a visionary intelligence-centric approach. The aim of CEI is to make intelligence a shareable and reusable first-class entity that can be independently represented, discovered, observed, exchanged, and managed across the distributed edge-cloud continuum. We present a three layer CEI architecture and examine enabling technologies and research dimensions, including intelligence inventories, semantic knowledge representation, communication, discoverability, observability, lifecycle automation, clustering mechanisms, marketplaces, interoperability, and standardization.

</details>


### [144] [PlanE: Meta Planning of Data, Tuning, and Inference for Extractive-based LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20470)
*Jiacheng Wang,Weiyan Zhang,Guangya Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了PlanE框架，通过数据分解、指令调优和提示推理构建抽取型大语言模型，并引入DTI规划器来选择最优基础模型和组合以提升构建效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增强大语言模型的特定任务能力需要大量指令调优数据，但海量数据的标注成本高昂，且缺乏针对特定任务优化LLM的方法。

Method: 提出了PlanE框架，包含三个核心步骤：数据分解、指令调优和提示推理；同时引入数据-调优-推理（DTI）规划器，用于为特定数据集选择最优的基础LLM及其DTI组合。

Result: 实验从两个角度验证了PlanE的有效性：（1）同一基础LLM在不同数据集上的表现；（2）不同基础LLM在同一数据集上的表现；并验证了DTI规划器在不同优化目标下的泛化能力。

Conclusion: PlanE框架能够有效降低构建任务特定LLM的数据标注成本，提升构建效率，DTI规划器具有良好的泛化性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Enhancing the task-specific capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) primarily requires substantial instruction-tuning datasets. However, the sheer volume of such data imposes a considerable annotation cost, and a lack of optimization methods for tailoring LLMs to specific tasks. To address the above issues, we propose a \textbf{Plan}ning framework for constructing \textbf{E}xtractive-based LLMs called \textbf{PlanE}, which includes data decomposition, instruction tuning, and prompt inference. Additionally, we introduce a Data-Tuning-Inference (DTI) planner, aimed at selecting the optimal base-LLM and its DTI combinations for specific datasets to improve construction efficiency. The experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of our PlanE from two views: (1) across different datasets using the same base-LLM, and (2) on the same dataset using different base-LLMs. Furthermore, we validate the generalizability of the proposed DTI planner under different optimization objectives. The codes are publicly available at https://github.com/gugugu-469/PlanE.

</details>


### [145] [Toward Continuous Assurance for the Democratization of AI Agent Creation in Industry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21495)
*Natan Levy,Harel Berger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对非工程用户创建的AI代理存在的可靠性问题，提出了一种轻量级的持续保障框架，通过依赖映射、就绪契约、定期检查和生命周期治理来确保代理的运行就绪状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着低代码、无代码和对话式开发环境的普及，组织内部的非工程用户可以创建AI代理，这种民主化带来了快速创新的便利，但也产生了可靠性差距：代理依赖于模型、工具、权限、提示等易变因素，可能导致部署后出现静默降级，即使没有用户直接修改代理。

Method: 提出一种轻量级持续保障框架，结合依赖映射、就绪契约、定期检查、诊断和生命周期治理来评估代理在预期条件下是否保持运行就绪状态，并实现了一个初始原型审计器。

Result: 通过原型审计器和基于场景的评估，展示了所提出的分类法如何转化为实际检查和可操作的修复指导。

Conclusion: 该框架能够帮助组织识别公民创建的AI代理的可靠性风险，并提供实用的检查和治理机制来确保代理的持续可靠运行。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly created inside organizations by non-engineering users through low-code, no-code, and conversational development environments. This democratization enables rapid local innovation, but it also creates a reliability gap: agents that appear to users as simple productivity artifacts may depend on changing models, tools, retrieval sources, permissions, prompts, schedules, and external services. These dependencies can cause silent degradation long after deployment, even when no user directly modifies the agent. This paper identifies the reliability challenge created by democratized AI agent creation and proposes a lightweight continuous-assurance framework for citizen-created organizational agents. The framework combines dependency mapping, readiness contracts, scheduled checks, diagnostics, and lifecycle governance to assess whether an agent remains operationally ready under expected conditions. We also present an initial prototype auditor and scenario-based assessment showing how the proposed taxonomy can be translated into practical checks and actionable remediation guidance.

</details>


### [146] [Benchmarking the Personalization Capabilities of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20471)
*Ashutosh Srivastava,Siddharth Yedlapati,Vinay Aggarwal,Yaman Kumar Singla,Shashwat Dixit,Jitendra Ajmera,Balaji Krishnamurthy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM在经典三方个性化场景下的表现，发布了包含6,279个客户成功案例的SDR-Bench基准，发现现有模型存在个性化性能瓶颈，在财富100强科技企业数据集上无法有效区分成功与失败的推广信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM个性化基准仅测量发送方适配（接收方即被服务用户），而经典三方个性化问题（生成消息能否诱导第三方采取预期行动）仅通过A/B测试和小规模人类研究考察，无法针对新模型按需重跑。LLM通过生成条件于推断接收方状态的连续消息变体消除了经典检索-排序方法的有界库存约束，因此需要评估当前模型在经典意义上的个性化能力。

Method: 将Kamenica和Gentzkow（2011）的贝叶斯说服框架适配至生成式智能体，在销售场景中实例化该形式化表述（接收方行动 routinely 对应诱导它们的推广活动进行日志记录）。发布SDR-Bench公开语料库（6,279个客户成功故事，覆盖22个行业、约200家企业），通过时间约束模拟防止未来数据泄露。与12名专业销售代表进行实地部署验证。

Result: 在前沿LLM和深度研究智能体中观察到一致的个性化性能瓶颈；在财富100强科技企业群体上，没有模型能够统计显著地区分成功与失败的推广信息。实地部署验证中，48%的模型生成内容被评为即时有用，高级专家一致性达Pearson 0.82。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在经典三方个性化任务上表现不佳，存在明显的性能瓶颈，公开发布SDR-Arena和SDR-Bench以支持大规模生成式个性化的可重复研究。

Abstract: Personalization, the act of varying a message to induce action from a specific receiver while keeping sender, channel, and time fixed, has a long tradition in psychology and marketing as a two-party problem in which sender and receiver have independent objectives. Large language models remove the bounded-inventory constraint of classical retrieval-and-ranking approaches by generating a continuum of message variants conditioned on inferred receiver state, raising the question of how well current models perform personalization in the classical sense. Existing LLM personalization benchmarks measure sender-side adaptation, in which the receiver is the same user the model is serving. The two-party question, whether a generated message induces its intended action in a third party, has been investigated only through A/B tests and small-scale human studies that cannot be re-run against a new model on demand. We adapt the Bayesian Persuasion framework of Kamenica and Gentzkow (2011) to generative agents and instantiate the formulation in sales, where receiver actions are routinely logged against the outreach that induced them. We release SDR-Bench, a public corpus of 6,279 customer success stories spanning 22 industries and approximately 200 enterprises, served through a temporally constrained simulation that prevents future-data leakage. Across frontier LLMs and deep-research agents, we observe a consistent personalization plateau and on a Fortune 100 tech cohort no model statistically separates successful from unsuccessful outreach. A field deployment with 12 professional sales representatives validates the framework, with 48 percent of model-generated content rated immediately useful and senior-expert agreement at Pearson 0.82. We release SDR-Arena and SDR-Bench publicly to support reproducible study of generative personalization at scale.

</details>


### [147] [The Boundaries of Automation: A Theory of Persistent Human Participation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21547)
*Fares Fourati,Hinrich Schütze,Eyke Hüllermeier,Iryna Gurevych*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文挑战了“AI能力提升终将取代人类参与”的自动化假设，提出人类参与在三类情况下会持续存在：技术互补性、规范与发展需求、以及目标涌现性，其中人类参与本身就是目标构成的一部分，而非执行缺陷的临时补丁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI发展基于一个隐含假设：人类之所以停留在任务回路中，仅因为当前AI能力不足，因此自动化应尽可能推进。作者认为该假设值得质疑，需探讨自动化的概念边界，以及人类参与在高度能力AI系统中仍将持续存在的原因。

Method: 采用概念分析与理论论证方法，从三个维度系统论证人类参与的持续必要性：技术或互补性理由（人类提供AI无法获得的能力与视角）；规范或发展性理由（参与本身对人类能动性与学习有价值）；涌现性理由（目标在交互过程中共同涌现，参与具有构成性作用）。

Result: 提出“人机协同建构”概念，指出在目标通过参与而涌现的活动中，人类参与并非改善执行的权宜之计，而是目标生成的构成要素；这意味着自动化存在概念边界，某些任务本质上需要人机协同完成。

Conclusion: 该视角对自动化边界的理解、未来AI系统的设计、评估与伦理具有重要启示，表明人机协同不应被视为过渡状态，而应作为特定活动类型的持久特征加以设计，这对AI发展路径与政策制定具有根本性意义。

Abstract: The rapid progress of AI has intensified the long-standing pursuit of automation: replacing human participation with algorithms wherever possible. Implicit in this pursuit is the assumption that humans remain in the loop only because current AI systems are not yet sufficiently capable. This paper challenges that assumption. Rather than asking how far automation can extend, we ask where its conceptual limits lie and argue that human participation may persist even with highly capable AI systems for three distinct reasons. Technical or complementarity grounds arise when humans contribute capabilities or perspectives unavailable to AI. Normative or developmental grounds arise when participation itself is valuable for human agency or learning.
